(
cat << 'EOF' > Motif/Sources/Motif/Services/SystemServices.swift
import Foundation
import AppKit
import Combine
import SQLite3
import ImageIO
import CryptoKit

// MARK: - Dedicated Ephemeral URLSession & RFC3339 Fractional-Seconds Date Parser
public enum OllamaNetworking {
    public static let ephemeralSession: URLSession = {
        let cfg = URLSessionConfiguration.ephemeral
        cfg.urlCache = nil
        cfg.requestCachePolicy = .reloadIgnoringLocalCacheData
        cfg.timeoutIntervalForRequest = 600.0
        cfg.timeoutIntervalForResource = 3600.0
        cfg.waitsForConnectivity = false
        return URLSession(configuration: cfg)
    }()

    public static func parseRFC3339Date(_ raw: String) -> Date? {
        let withFrac = ISO8601DateFormatter()
        withFrac.formatOptions = [.withInternetDateTime, .withFractionalSeconds]
        if let d = withFrac.date(from: raw) { return d }
        let plain = ISO8601DateFormatter()
        plain.formatOptions = [.withInternetDateTime]
        return plain.date(from: raw)
    }
}

// MARK: - External Models Directory Manager (Zero Thread-Unsafe setenv Calls!)
public final class ExternalStorageDriveManager: ObservableObject {
    @Published public var externalVolumePath: String = ""
    @Published public var isExternalDriveConnected: Bool = true
    private let bookmarkDefaultsKey = "MotifExternalDriveBookmarkDataV8"
    private let pathStringDefaultsKey = "MotifExternalDrivePathStringV8"

    public init() {
        restoreSecurityScopedBookmark()
        if externalVolumePath.isEmpty {
            Task.detached(priority: .utility) {
                if let discovered = Self.discoverPathFromShellOrLaunchctl() {
                    let resolved = Self.resolveActualOllamaModelsRoot(fromPath: discovered)
                    let exists = FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: resolved)
                    await MainActor.run { [weak self] in
                        self?.externalVolumePath = resolved
                        self?.isExternalDriveConnected = exists
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public var hasModelsOnDisk: Bool {
        let path = resolvedModelsPath
        guard !path.isEmpty else { return false }
        let fm = FileManager.default
        return fm.fileExists(atPath: (path as NSString).appendingPathComponent("manifests"))
            || fm.fileExists(atPath: (path as NSString).appendingPathComponent("blobs"))
    }

    public var resolvedModelsPath: String {
        guard !externalVolumePath.isEmpty else { return "" }
        return Self.resolveActualOllamaModelsRoot(fromPath: externalVolumePath)
    }

    @discardableResult
    public func selectExternalDriveDirectory() -> Bool {
        let panel = NSOpenPanel()
        panel.title = "Select External Ollama Models Directory"
        panel.message = "Choose your external drive's Ollama models folder (containing 'manifests' and 'blobs')."
        panel.canChooseDirectories = true
        panel.canChooseFiles = false
        panel.canCreateDirectories = true
        panel.allowsMultipleSelection = false
        panel.showsHiddenFiles = true

        if panel.runModal() == .OK, let url = panel.url {
            _ = url.startAccessingSecurityScopedResource()
            let resolvedPath = Self.resolveActualOllamaModelsRoot(fromPath: url.path)
            if let bookmarkData = try? url.bookmarkData(options: [.withSecurityScope], includingResourceValuesForKeys: nil, relativeTo: nil) {
                UserDefaults.standard.set(bookmarkData, forKey: bookmarkDefaultsKey)
            }
            UserDefaults.standard.set(resolvedPath, forKey: pathStringDefaultsKey)
            externalVolumePath = resolvedPath
            isExternalDriveConnected = FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: resolvedPath)
            return true
        }
        return false
    }

    private func restoreSecurityScopedBookmark() {
        if let data = UserDefaults.standard.data(forKey: bookmarkDefaultsKey) {
            var isStale = false
            if let url = try? URL(resolvingBookmarkData: data, options: [.withSecurityScope], relativeTo: nil, bookmarkDataIsStale: &isStale) {
                _ = url.startAccessingSecurityScopedResource()
                let savedSubpath = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: pathStringDefaultsKey) ?? url.path
                externalVolumePath = Self.resolveActualOllamaModelsRoot(fromPath: savedSubpath)
                isExternalDriveConnected = FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: externalVolumePath)
                return
            }
        }
        if let savedPath = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: pathStringDefaultsKey), !savedPath.isEmpty {
            externalVolumePath = Self.resolveActualOllamaModelsRoot(fromPath: savedPath)
            isExternalDriveConnected = FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: externalVolumePath)
        }
    }

    public static func resolveActualOllamaModelsRoot(fromPath rawPath: String) -> String {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        let base = (rawPath as NSString).expandingTildeInPath
        let candidates = [
            base,
            (base as NSString).appendingPathComponent(".ollama/models"),
            (base as NSString).appendingPathComponent("models"),
            (base as NSString).appendingPathComponent("ollama/models"),
            (base as NSString).appendingPathComponent("Ollama/models")
        ]
        for candidate in candidates {
            if fm.fileExists(atPath: (candidate as NSString).appendingPathComponent("manifests"))
                || fm.fileExists(atPath: (candidate as NSString).appendingPathComponent("blobs")) {
                return candidate
            }
        }
        return base
    }

    public static func discoverPathFromShellOrLaunchctl() -> String? {
        if let envVal = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["OLLAMA_MODELS"],
           !envVal.isEmpty, FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: envVal) {
            return envVal
        }
        let pipe = Pipe()
        let lcProc = Process()
        lcProc.executableURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: "/bin/launchctl")
        lcProc.arguments = ["getenv", "OLLAMA_MODELS"]
        lcProc.standardOutput = pipe
        lcProc.standardError = FileHandle.nullDevice
        if (try? lcProc.run()) != nil {
            lcProc.waitUntilExit()
            let data = pipe.fileHandleForReading.readDataToEndOfFile()
            if let str = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8)?.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines),
               !str.isEmpty, FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: str) {
                return str
            }
        }
        let home = FileManager.default.homeDirectoryForCurrentUser.path
        for file in [".zshrc", ".zprofile", ".zshenv", ".bash_profile", ".bashrc"] {
            let fullPath = (home as NSString).appendingPathComponent(file)
            guard let content = try? String(contentsOfFile: fullPath, encoding: .utf8) else { continue }
            for line in content.components(separatedBy: .newlines) {
                let trimmed = line.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
                guard !trimmed.hasPrefix("#"), trimmed.contains("OLLAMA_MODELS=") else { continue }
                if let eqRange = trimmed.range(of: "OLLAMA_MODELS=") {
                    var val = String(trimmed[eqRange.upperBound...]).trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
                    if let commentIdx = val.firstIndex(of: "#") {
                        val = String(val[..<commentIdx]).trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
                    }
                    val = val.trimmingCharacters(in: CharacterSet(charactersIn: "\"'"))
                    let expanded = (val as NSString).expandingTildeInPath
                    if !expanded.isEmpty && FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: expanded) {
                        return expanded
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return nil
    }
}

// MARK: - Daemon Ownership Mode (Strictly Separates External vs Managed Instances)
public enum OllamaDaemonMode: String, Equatable, Sendable {
    case offline = "Offline"
    case external = "External Daemon"
    case managedByMotif = "Managed by Motif"
}

// MARK: - Crash-Safe Local Engine Supervisor (Parent-Death Watchdog + Rolling Stderr Log + No Hostile killall)
@MainActor
public final class LocalEngineSupervisor: ObservableObject {
    public static let defaultLoopbackURL: String = "http://127.0.0.1:11434"

    @Published public var isEngineOnline: Bool = false
    @Published public var daemonMode: OllamaDaemonMode = .offline
    @Published public var ollamaVersionString: String = ""
    @Published public var daemonLogLines: [String] = []

    @Published public var engineEndpoint: String {
        didSet { UserDefaults.standard.set(Self.sanitizeEndpointString(engineEndpoint), forKey: "MotifEngineEndpointV10") }
    }
    @Published public var bindToLocalNetwork: Bool {
        didSet { UserDefaults.standard.set(bindToLocalNetwork, forKey: "MotifBindNetworkV10") }
    }
    @Published public var airGappedNoCloud: Bool {
        didSet { UserDefaults.standard.set(airGappedNoCloud, forKey: "MotifNoCloudV14") }
    }
    @Published public var parallelRequestSlots: Int {
        didSet { UserDefaults.standard.set(parallelRequestSlots, forKey: "MotifParallelSlotsV10") }
    }
    @Published public var maxLoadedModels: Int {
        didSet { UserDefaults.standard.set(maxLoadedModels, forKey: "MotifMaxLoadedModelsV10") }
    }
    @Published public var enableFlashAttentionEnv: Bool {
        didSet { UserDefaults.standard.set(enableFlashAttentionEnv, forKey: "MotifFlashAttnV10") }
    }
    @Published public var kvCacheTypeEnv: KVCacheQuantization {
        didSet { UserDefaults.standard.set(kvCacheTypeEnv.rawValue, forKey: "MotifKVCacheTypeV10") }
    }
    @Published public var autoUnloadOnSleep: Bool {
        didSet { UserDefaults.standard.set(autoUnloadOnSleep, forKey: "MotifAutoUnloadSleepV14") }
    }
    @Published public var autoUnloadOnQuit: Bool {
        didSet { UserDefaults.standard.set(autoUnloadOnQuit, forKey: "MotifAutoUnloadQuitV14") }
    }
    @Published public var useThinkingForSummary: Bool {
        didSet { UserDefaults.standard.set(useThinkingForSummary, forKey: "MotifUseThinkingForSummaryV10") }
    }
    @Published public var supervisorStatusLog: String = "Checking local Ollama daemon…"

    public var isManagedByMotif: Bool { daemonMode == .managedByMotif }

    private var managedProcess: Process?
    private var logPipe: Pipe?
    private var terminationObserver: NSObjectProtocol?

    public static func sanitizeEndpointString(_ raw: String) -> String {
        var cleaned = raw.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        while cleaned.hasSuffix("/") { cleaned.removeLast() }
        if cleaned.isEmpty { return defaultLoopbackURL }
        if !cleaned.lowercased().hasPrefix("http://") && !cleaned.lowercased().hasPrefix("https://") {
            cleaned = "http://" + cleaned
        }
        return cleaned
    }

    public var normalizedEndpoint: String { Self.sanitizeEndpointString(engineEndpoint) }

    public init() {
        let rawSaved = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: "MotifEngineEndpointV10") ?? Self.defaultLoopbackURL
        self.engineEndpoint = Self.sanitizeEndpointString(rawSaved)
        self.bindToLocalNetwork = UserDefaults.standard.bool(forKey: "MotifBindNetworkV10")
        self.airGappedNoCloud = UserDefaults.standard.bool(forKey: "MotifNoCloudV14")
        let savedParallel = UserDefaults.standard.integer(forKey: "MotifParallelSlotsV10")
        self.parallelRequestSlots = savedParallel > 0 ? savedParallel : 1
        let savedMaxModels = UserDefaults.standard.integer(forKey: "MotifMaxLoadedModelsV10")
        self.maxLoadedModels = savedMaxModels > 0 ? savedMaxModels : 1
        if UserDefaults.standard.object(forKey: "MotifFlashAttnV10") != nil {
            self.enableFlashAttentionEnv = UserDefaults.standard.bool(forKey: "MotifFlashAttnV10")
        } else {
            self.enableFlashAttentionEnv = true
        }
        let savedKV = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: "MotifKVCacheTypeV10") ?? KVCacheQuantization.q8_0.rawValue
        self.kvCacheTypeEnv = KVCacheQuantization(rawValue: savedKV) ?? .q8_0
        self.autoUnloadOnSleep = UserDefaults.standard.bool(forKey: "MotifAutoUnloadSleepV14")
        self.autoUnloadOnQuit = UserDefaults.standard.bool(forKey: "MotifAutoUnloadQuitV14")
        self.useThinkingForSummary = UserDefaults.standard.bool(forKey: "MotifUseThinkingForSummaryV10")

        terminationObserver = NotificationCenter.default.addObserver(
            forName: NSApplication.willTerminateNotification,
            object: nil,
            queue: .main
        ) { [weak self] _ in
            Task { @MainActor in
                self?.terminateManagedDaemonGracefully()
            }
        }
    }

    deinit {
        if let obs = terminationObserver { NotificationCenter.default.removeObserver(obs) }
    }

    public func findOllamaBinaryPath() -> String? {
        let candidates = [
            "/opt/homebrew/bin/ollama",
            "/usr/local/bin/ollama",
            "/Applications/Ollama.app/Contents/Resources/ollama",
            "\(FileManager.default.homeDirectoryForCurrentUser.path)/.ollama/bin/ollama"
        ]
        return candidates.first(where: { FileManager.default.isExecutableFile(atPath: $0) })
    }

    public func appendLog(_ line: String) {
        let trimmed = line.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard !trimmed.isEmpty else { return }
        daemonLogLines.append(trimmed)
        if daemonLogLines.count > 200 {
            daemonLogLines.removeFirst(daemonLogLines.count - 200)
        }
    }

    /// Probes `GET /api/version` as the authoritative liveness check.
    @discardableResult
    public func probeVersion() async -> Bool {
        guard let url = URL(string: "\(normalizedEndpoint)/api/version") else { return false }
        var req = URLRequest(url: url)
        req.timeoutInterval = 2.5
        do {
            let (data, resp) = try await OllamaNetworking.ephemeralSession.data(for: req)
            guard let http = resp as? HTTPURLResponse, http.statusCode == 200 else {
                isEngineOnline = false
                if managedProcess == nil { daemonMode = .offline }
                return false
            }
            if let json = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any],
               let ver = json["version"] as? String {
                ollamaVersionString = ver
            }
            isEngineOnline = true
            if managedProcess?.isRunning == true {
                daemonMode = .managedByMotif
            } else {
                daemonMode = .external
            }
            return true
        } catch {
            isEngineOnline = false
            if managedProcess == nil { daemonMode = .offline }
            return false
        }
    }

    /// Launches a crash-safe managed `ollama serve` with a POSIX parent-death watchdog & rolling stderr capture.
    public func launchManagedDaemonIfNeeded(externalModelsDir: String = "") async {
        if let running = managedProcess, running.isRunning {
            _ = await probeVersion()
            return
        }
        if await probeVersion() {
            daemonMode = .external
            supervisorStatusLog = "Attached to external Ollama \(ollamaVersionString) at \(normalizedEndpoint)."
            return
        }
        guard let executablePath = findOllamaBinaryPath() else {
            supervisorStatusLog = "Ollama binary not found. Install Ollama or check your endpoint in Settings."
            return
        }

        let resolvedDir = externalModelsDir.isEmpty
            ? (ExternalStorageDriveManager.discoverPathFromShellOrLaunchctl() ?? "")
            : ExternalStorageDriveManager.resolveActualOllamaModelsRoot(fromPath: externalModelsDir)

        let parentPID = ProcessInfo.processInfo.processIdentifier
        let wrapperScript = """
        trap 'kill -TERM "$CHILD" 2>/dev/null; wait "$CHILD" 2>/dev/null; exit 0' TERM INT HUP
        "$OLLAMA_BIN_PATH" serve &
        CHILD=$!
        while kill -0 "$MOTIF_PARENT_PID" 2>/dev/null && kill -0 "$CHILD" 2>/dev/null; do
          sleep 2
        done
        kill -TERM "$CHILD" 2>/dev/null
        wait "$CHILD" 2>/dev/null
        """

        let proc = Process()
        proc.executableURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: "/bin/sh")
        proc.arguments = ["-c", wrapperScript]

        var env = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment
        env["OLLAMA_BIN_PATH"] = executablePath
        env["MOTIF_PARENT_PID"] = "\(parentPID)"
        env["OLLAMA_HOST"] = bindToLocalNetwork ? "0.0.0.0:11434" : "127.0.0.1:11434"
        env["OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL"] = "\(max(1, parallelRequestSlots))"
        env["OLLAMA_MAX_LOADED_MODELS"] = "\(max(1, maxLoadedModels))"
        let flashRequired = enableFlashAttentionEnv || kvCacheTypeEnv != .f16
        env["OLLAMA_FLASH_ATTENTION"] = flashRequired ? "1" : "0"
        env["OLLAMA_KV_CACHE_TYPE"] = kvCacheTypeEnv.rawValue
        if airGappedNoCloud { env["OLLAMA_NO_CLOUD"] = "1" }
        if !resolvedDir.isEmpty { env["OLLAMA_MODELS"] = resolvedDir }
        proc.environment = env

        let pipe = Pipe()
        proc.standardOutput = pipe
        proc.standardError = pipe
        logPipe = pipe

        pipe.fileHandleForReading.readabilityHandler = { [weak self] handle in
            let data = handle.availableData
            if data.isEmpty {
                handle.readabilityHandler = nil
                return
            }
            if let chunk = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8) {
                let lines = chunk.components(separatedBy: .newlines)
                Task { @MainActor [weak self] in
                    lines.forEach { self?.appendLog($0) }
                }
            }
        }

        proc.terminationHandler = { [weak self] exitedProc in
            Task { @MainActor [weak self] in
                guard let self = self else { return }
                if self.managedProcess === exitedProc {
                    self.logPipe?.fileHandleForReading.readabilityHandler = nil
                    self.logPipe = nil
                    self.managedProcess = nil
                    self.daemonMode = .offline
                    self.isEngineOnline = false
                    self.appendLog("[Motif] Managed Ollama process exited (status \(exitedProc.terminationStatus)).")
                }
            }
        }

        do {
            try proc.run()
            managedProcess = proc
            daemonMode = .managedByMotif
            supervisorStatusLog = "Starting Motif-managed Ollama…"
            for _ in 0..<15 {
                try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 250_000_000)
                if await probeVersion() {
                    let pathLabel = resolvedDir.isEmpty ? "~/.ollama/models" : resolvedDir
                    supervisorStatusLog = "Managed Ollama \(ollamaVersionString) · KV \(kvCacheTypeEnv.rawValue.uppercased()) · Parallel \(parallelRequestSlots) · \(pathLabel)"
                    break
                }
            }
        } catch {
            supervisorStatusLog = "Failed to start Ollama: \(error.localizedDescription)"
            appendLog("[Motif] Launch failed: \(error.localizedDescription)")
        }
    }

    /// Safe restart: ONLY restarts Motif's own managed daemon. Never kills external daemons without explicit user takeover.
    public func restartManagedDaemonOnly(modelsPath: String) async -> Bool {
        guard daemonMode == .managedByMotif || managedProcess != nil else {
            return false
        }
        terminateManagedDaemonGracefully()
        try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 650_000_000)
        await launchManagedDaemonIfNeeded(externalModelsDir: modelsPath)
        return isEngineOnline
    }

    public func terminateManagedDaemonGracefully() {
        logPipe?.fileHandleForReading.readabilityHandler = nil
        logPipe = nil
        guard let proc = managedProcess else { return }
        managedProcess = nil
        daemonMode = .offline
        isEngineOnline = false
        supervisorStatusLog = "Managed Ollama daemon stopped."
        if proc.isRunning {
            proc.terminate()
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Real SQLite WAL Vault + Transactional FTS5 + Corrupt-File Protection + Retina-Safe Assets & GC
public final class PersistenceVaultManager {
    private let vaultDirectory: URL
    private let assetsDirectory: URL
    private let sqliteURL: URL
    private var db: OpaquePointer? = nil
    private let dbQueue = DispatchQueue(label: "com.motif.studio.sqlite.serial", qos: .utility)
    private var debounceWorkItem: DispatchWorkItem?
    public private(set) var lastRecoveryNotice: String? = nil

    public init() {
        let appSupport = FileManager.default.urls(for: .applicationSupportDirectory, in: .userDomainMask).first!
        vaultDirectory = appSupport.appendingPathComponent("Motif", isDirectory: true)
        assetsDirectory = vaultDirectory.appendingPathComponent("HeavyAssets", isDirectory: true)
        sqliteURL = vaultDirectory.appendingPathComponent("motif_vault_v14.sqlite3")

        try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: vaultDirectory, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: assetsDirectory, withIntermediateDirectories: true)

        dbQueue.sync {
            self.openAndConfigureSQLiteWAL()
        }
    }

    deinit {
        if let handle = db { sqlite3_close(handle) }
    }

    private func openAndConfigureSQLiteWAL() {
        guard sqlite3_open(sqliteURL.path, &db) == SQLITE_OK else { return }
        sqlite3_exec(db, "PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;", nil, nil, nil)
        sqlite3_exec(db, "PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL;", nil, nil, nil)
        sqlite3_exec(db, """
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
                updated_at REAL NOT NULL,
                is_trashed INTEGER NOT NULL,
                payload BLOB NOT NULL
            );
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS app_meta (
                key TEXT PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
                payload BLOB NOT NULL
            );
            CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages_fts USING fts5(
                session_id UNINDEXED,
                message_id UNINDEXED,
                session_title,
                content
            );
        """, nil, nil, nil)
    }

    public func scheduleDebouncedSave(
        sessions: [ConversationSession],
        folders: [ProjectFolder],
        presets: [ParameterPreset],
        personas: [SlashPersona]
    ) {
        debounceWorkItem?.cancel()
        let work = DispatchWorkItem { [weak self] in
            self?.writeAllInSingleTransaction(sessions: sessions, folders: folders, presets: presets, personas: personas)
        }
        debounceWorkItem = work
        dbQueue.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.30, execute: work)
    }

    private func writeAllInSingleTransaction(
        sessions: [ConversationSession],
        folders: [ProjectFolder],
        presets: [ParameterPreset],
        personas: [SlashPersona]
    ) {
        guard let db = db else { return }
        let encoder = JSONEncoder()
        encoder.dateEncodingStrategy = .iso8601
        let SQLITE_TRANSIENT = unsafeBitCast(-1, to: sqlite3_destructor_type.self)

        guard sqlite3_exec(db, "BEGIN IMMEDIATE;", nil, nil, nil) == SQLITE_OK else { return }

        // 1. Sync sessions table
        sqlite3_exec(db, "DELETE FROM sessions;", nil, nil, nil)
        var sessStmt: OpaquePointer? = nil
        if sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, "INSERT INTO sessions (id, updated_at, is_trashed, payload) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?);", -1, &sessStmt, nil) == SQLITE_OK {
            for session in sessions {
                guard let data = try? encoder.encode(session) else { continue }
                sqlite3_reset(sessStmt)
                sqlite3_bind_text(sessStmt, 1, (session.id.uuidString as NSString).utf8String, -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT)
                sqlite3_bind_double(sessStmt, 2, session.updatedAt.timeIntervalSince1970)
                sqlite3_bind_int(sessStmt, 3, session.isTrashed ? 1 : 0)
                data.withUnsafeBytes { rawBuf in
                    sqlite3_bind_blob(sessStmt, 4, rawBuf.baseAddress, Int32(data.count), SQLITE_TRANSIENT)
                }
                sqlite3_step(sessStmt)
            }
            sqlite3_finalize(sessStmt)
        }

        // 2. Sync app_meta (folders, presets, personas)
        var metaStmt: OpaquePointer? = nil
        if sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO app_meta (key, payload) VALUES (?, ?);", -1, &metaStmt, nil) == SQLITE_OK {
            let metaItems: [(String, Data?)] = [
                ("folders", try? encoder.encode(folders)),
                ("presets", try? encoder.encode(presets)),
                ("personas", try? encoder.encode(personas))
            ]
            for (k, maybeData) in metaItems {
                guard let data = maybeData else { continue }
                sqlite3_reset(metaStmt)
                sqlite3_bind_text(metaStmt, 1, (k as NSString).utf8String, -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT)
                data.withUnsafeBytes { rawBuf in
                    sqlite3_bind_blob(metaStmt, 2, rawBuf.baseAddress, Int32(data.count), SQLITE_TRANSIENT)
                }
                sqlite3_step(metaStmt)
            }
            sqlite3_finalize(metaStmt)
        }

        // 3. Rebuild FTS5 inside the SAME atomic transaction (1 fsync, never O(N) commits)
        sqlite3_exec(db, "DELETE FROM messages_fts;", nil, nil, nil)
        var ftsStmt: OpaquePointer? = nil
        if sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, "INSERT INTO messages_fts (session_id, message_id, session_title, content) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?);", -1, &ftsStmt, nil) == SQLITE_OK {
            for session in sessions where !session.isTrashed {
                for node in session.activeBranchThread where !node.content.isEmpty {
                    sqlite3_reset(ftsStmt)
                    sqlite3_bind_text(ftsStmt, 1, (session.id.uuidString as NSString).utf8String, -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT)
                    sqlite3_bind_text(ftsStmt, 2, (node.id.uuidString as NSString).utf8String, -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT)
                    sqlite3_bind_text(ftsStmt, 3, (session.title as NSString).utf8String, -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT)
                    sqlite3_bind_text(ftsStmt, 4, (node.content as NSString).utf8String, -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT)
                    sqlite3_step(ftsStmt)
                }
            }
            sqlite3_finalize(ftsStmt)
        }

        sqlite3_exec(db, "COMMIT;", nil, nil, nil)
    }

    /// Fully asynchronous FTS5 search — never blocks MainActor!
    public func searchMessagesFTS5Async(query: String) async -> [FTSSearchHit] {
        let cleaned = query
            .components(separatedBy: CharacterSet.alphanumerics.inverted)
            .filter { !$0.isEmpty }
            .map { "\($0)*" }
            .joined(separator: " AND ")
        guard !cleaned.isEmpty else { return [] }

        return await withCheckedContinuation { continuation in
            dbQueue.async { [weak self] in
                guard let self = self, let db = self.db else {
                    continuation.resume(returning: [])
                    return
                }
                let sql = "SELECT session_id, message_id, session_title, snippet(messages_fts, 3, '', '', '…', 14) FROM messages_fts WHERE messages_fts MATCH ? ORDER BY bm25(messages_fts) LIMIT 20;"
                var stmt: OpaquePointer? = nil
                guard sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, sql, -1, &stmt, nil) == SQLITE_OK else {
                    continuation.resume(returning: [])
                    return
                }
                defer { sqlite3_finalize(stmt) }

                let SQLITE_TRANSIENT = unsafeBitCast(-1, to: sqlite3_destructor_type.self)
                sqlite3_bind_text(stmt, 1, (cleaned as NSString).utf8String, -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT)

                var hits: [FTSSearchHit] = []
                while sqlite3_step(stmt) == SQLITE_ROW {
                    guard let sIDPtr = sqlite3_column_text(stmt, 0),
                          let mIDPtr = sqlite3_column_text(stmt, 1),
                          let titlePtr = sqlite3_column_text(stmt, 2),
                          let snipPtr = sqlite3_column_text(stmt, 3),
                          let sUUID = UUID(uuidString: String(cString: sIDPtr)),
                          let mUUID = UUID(uuidString: String(cString: mIDPtr)) else { continue }
                    hits.append(
                        FTSSearchHit(
                            sessionID: sUUID,
                            messageID: mUUID,
                            sessionTitle: String(cString: titlePtr),
                            snippetText: String(cString: snipPtr).replacingOccurrences(of: "\n", with: " ")
                        )
                    )
                }
                continuation.resume(returning: hits)
            }
        }
    }

    public func loadSavedState() -> ([ConversationSession], [ProjectFolder], [ParameterPreset], [SlashPersona])? {
        let decoder = JSONDecoder()
        decoder.dateDecodingStrategy = .iso8601

        var loadedSessions: [ConversationSession] = []
        var loadedFolders: [ProjectFolder] = []
        var loadedPresets: [ParameterPreset] = ParameterPreset.defaults
        var loadedPersonas: [SlashPersona] = SlashPersona.defaults
        var foundInSQLite = false

        dbQueue.sync {
            guard let db = self.db else { return }
            var stmt: OpaquePointer? = nil
            if sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, "SELECT payload FROM sessions ORDER BY updated_at DESC;", -1, &stmt, nil) == SQLITE_OK {
                while sqlite3_step(stmt) == SQLITE_ROW {
                    if let bytes = sqlite3_column_blob(stmt, 0) {
                        let len = Int(sqlite3_column_bytes(stmt, 0))
                        let data = Data(bytes: bytes, count: len)
                        if let sess = try? decoder.decode(ConversationSession.self, from: data) {
                            loadedSessions.append(sess)
                            foundInSQLite = true
                        }
                    }
                }
                sqlite3_finalize(stmt)
            }

            var mStmt: OpaquePointer? = nil
            if sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, "SELECT key, payload FROM app_meta;", -1, &mStmt, nil) == SQLITE_OK {
                while sqlite3_step(mStmt) == SQLITE_ROW {
                    guard let kPtr = sqlite3_column_text(mStmt, 0),
                          let bytes = sqlite3_column_blob(mStmt, 1) else { continue }
                    let key = String(cString: kPtr)
                    let data = Data(bytes: bytes, count: Int(sqlite3_column_bytes(mStmt, 1)))
                    if key == "folders", let f = try? decoder.decode([ProjectFolder].self, from: data) { loadedFolders = f; foundInSQLite = true }
                    if key == "presets", let p = try? decoder.decode([ParameterPreset].self, from: data) { loadedPresets = p; foundInSQLite = true }
                    if key == "personas", let sp = try? decoder.decode([SlashPersona].self, from: data) { loadedPersonas = sp; foundInSQLite = true }
                }
                sqlite3_finalize(mStmt)
            }
        }

        if foundInSQLite {
            return (loadedSessions, loadedFolders, loadedPresets, loadedPersonas)
        }

        // Transparent migration from legacy v8 JSON (with corrupt-file backup so history is NEVER silently wiped!)
        let legacySessionsURL = vaultDirectory.appendingPathComponent("sessions_v8.json")
        if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: legacySessionsURL.path) {
            if let sData = try? Data(contentsOf: legacySessionsURL),
               let sessions = try? decoder.decode([ConversationSession].self, from: sData) {
                let folders = (try? Data(contentsOf: vaultDirectory.appendingPathComponent("folders_v8.json")))
                    .flatMap { try? decoder.decode([ProjectFolder].self, from: $0) } ?? []
                let presets = (try? Data(contentsOf: vaultDirectory.appendingPathComponent("presets_v8.json")))
                    .flatMap { try? decoder.decode([ParameterPreset].self, from: $0) } ?? ParameterPreset.defaults
                let personas = (try? Data(contentsOf: vaultDirectory.appendingPathComponent("personas_v8.json")))
                    .flatMap { try? decoder.decode([SlashPersona].self, from: $0) } ?? SlashPersona.defaults
                scheduleDebouncedSave(sessions: sessions, folders: folders, presets: presets, personas: personas)
                return (sessions, folders, presets, personas)
            } else {
                let ts = Int(Date().timeIntervalSince1970)
                let backupURL = vaultDirectory.appendingPathComponent("sessions_v8_corrupt_\(ts).json")
                try? FileManager.default.moveItem(at: legacySessionsURL, to: backupURL)
                lastRecoveryNotice = "Previous sessions_v8.json could not be parsed and was safely backed up to \(backupURL.lastPathComponent)."
            }
        }
        return nil
    }

    // MARK: - Content-Addressed Heavy Assets (SHA-256) + Garbage Collection
    public func persistHeavyAsset(data: Data, preferredExtension: String) -> String {
        let digest = SHA256.hash(data: data).map { String(format: "%02x", $0) }.joined()
        let ext = preferredExtension.isEmpty ? "dat" : preferredExtension
        let filename = "sha_\(digest.prefix(24)).\(ext)"
        let fileURL = assetsDirectory.appendingPathComponent(filename)
        if !FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: fileURL.path) {
            try? data.write(to: fileURL, options: [.atomic])
        }
        return filename
    }

    public func loadHeavyAssetText(filename: String) -> String? {
        let fileURL = assetsDirectory.appendingPathComponent(filename)
        guard let data = try? Data(contentsOf: fileURL) else { return nil }
        return String(data: data, encoding: .utf8)
    }

    public func loadHeavyAssetBase64(filename: String) -> String? {
        let fileURL = assetsDirectory.appendingPathComponent(filename)
        guard let data = try? Data(contentsOf: fileURL) else { return nil }
        return data.base64EncodedString()
    }

    /// Pixel-exact ImageIO downscaling (no Retina 2x bloat from deprecated NSImage.lockFocus!).
    public func downscaleAndPersistVisionImage(data: Data) -> (filename: String, base64JPEG: String, byteCount: Int)? {
        guard let source = CGImageSourceCreateWithData(data as CFData, nil) else { return nil }
        let options: [CFString: Any] = [
            kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailFromImageAlways: true,
            kCGImageSourceShouldCacheImmediately: true,
            kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailWithTransform: true,
            kCGImageSourceThumbnailMaxPixelSize: 1568
        ]
        guard let cgImage = CGImageSourceCreateThumbnailAtIndex(source, 0, options as CFDictionary) else {
            let fallbackName = persistHeavyAsset(data: data, preferredExtension: "img")
            return (fallbackName, data.base64EncodedString(), data.count)
        }
        let rep = NSBitmapImageRep(cgImage: cgImage)
        guard let jpegData = rep.representation(using: .jpeg, properties: [.compressionFactor: 0.82]) else {
            let fallbackName = persistHeavyAsset(data: data, preferredExtension: "img")
            return (fallbackName, data.base64EncodedString(), data.count)
        }
        let savedName = persistHeavyAsset(data: jpegData, preferredExtension: "jpg")
        return (savedName, jpegData.base64EncodedString(), jpegData.count)
    }

    public func garbageCollectUnreferencedAssets(referencedFilenames: Set<String>) {
        dbQueue.async { [assetsDirectory] in
            guard let files = try? FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(at: assetsDirectory, includingPropertiesForKeys: nil) else { return }
            for file in files where !referencedFilenames.contains(file.lastPathComponent) {
                try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: file)
            }
        }
    }
}
EOF
)

    // MARK: - Composer, Scroll & Stop-Safe Streaming State
    @Published public var composerDraft: String = ""
    @Published public var pendingAttachments: [MessageAttachment] = []
    @Published public var isStreamingGeneration: Bool = false
    @Published public var streamingSessionID: UUID? = nil
    @Published public var streamingNodeID: UUID? = nil
    @Published public var liveTokensPerSec: Double = 0.0
    @Published public var liveTotalTokens: Int = 0
    @Published public var liveThinkingTokens: Int = 0
    @Published public var liveElapsedSeconds: Double = 0.0
    @Published public var streamScrollPulse: Int = 0
    @Published public var isScrolledAwayFromBottom: Bool = false
    @Published public var isAutoScrollPaused: Bool = false
    @Published public var humanReadableErrorBanner: String? = nil
    @Published public var kernelMemoryPressureWarning: String? = nil
    @Published public var subtleStatusPillMessage: String? = nil

    // MARK: - Models, Memory Telemetry & Downloads
    @Published public var availableModels: [LocalModelDescriptor] = []
    @Published public var liveOllamaTotalBytes: Int64 = 0
    @Published public var baselineOllamaRSSBeforeLoad: Int64 = 0
    @Published public var baselineRAMBytesBeforeLoad: Int64 = 0
    @Published public var targetLoadingModelBytes: Int64 = 0
    @Published public var memorySnapshot: UnifiedMemorySnapshot
    @Published public var isLoadingModelWeights: Bool = false
    @Published public var isEvaluatingConversationContext: Bool = false
    @Published public var loadingModelName: String = ""
    @Published public var loadingModelInternalID: String = ""
    @Published public var isConfirmingModelLoadCancel: Bool = false
    @Published public var modelLoadCompletionRimFlashOpacity: Double = 0.0
    @Published public var pendingSSDSwapWarningModel: LocalModelDescriptor? = nil
    @Published public var downloadTasks: [RegistryDownloadTask] = []

    public var isApplyingProgrammaticModelSwitch: Bool = false
    private var modelsLoadedByMotif = Set<String>()

    // MARK: - Sub-Services
    @Published public var supervisor = LocalEngineSupervisor()
    @Published public var driveManager = ExternalStorageDriveManager()

    private let vault = PersistenceVaultManager()
    private var activeGenerationID: UUID? = nil
    private var streamingTask: Task<Void, Never>? = nil
    private var modelLoadTask: Task<Void, Never>? = nil
    private var downloadTaskHandles: [UUID: Task<Void, Never>] = [:]
    private var activeStreamParser: StatefulThinkingStreamParser? = nil
    private var telemetryTask: Task<Void, Never>? = nil
    private var isTelemetryQueryInFlight: Bool = false
    private var scrollWheelMonitor: Any? = nil
    private var memoryPressureSource: DispatchSourceMemoryPressure? = nil
    private var appObservers: [NSObjectProtocol] = []
    private var workspaceObservers: [NSObjectProtocol] = []
    private var cancellables = Set<AnyCancellable>()
    private var perModelParamsSaveWorkItem: DispatchWorkItem? = nil
    private var lastStreamUIUpdateTime: Date = .distantPast
    private var appNapActivity: NSObjectProtocol? = nil
    private var lastStreamActivityTime: Date = .distantPast
    private var streamWatchdogTask: Task<Void, Never>? = nil
    private var searchTask: Task<Void, Never>? = nil

    public var currentTokens: MotifTokens {
        MotifTokens(theme: activeTheme, customPalettes: customPalettes, isWindowActive: isAppActive)
    }

    public init() {
        let savedThemeRaw = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: "MotifActiveThemeV8") ?? AppThemeID.alabasterStudio.rawValue
        self.activeTheme = AppThemeID(rawValue: savedThemeRaw) ?? .alabasterStudio

        if let palData = UserDefaults.standard.data(forKey: "MotifCustomPalettesV11"),
           let decodedPal = try? JSONDecoder().decode(CustomStudioPalettes.self, from: palData) {
            self.customPalettes = decodedPal
        } else {
            self.customPalettes = CustomStudioPalettes()
        }

        if let pmData = UserDefaults.standard.data(forKey: "MotifPerModelParamsV14") ?? UserDefaults.standard.data(forKey: "MotifPerModelParamsV12"),
           let decodedPM = try? JSONDecoder().decode([String: ModelRuntimeParameters].self, from: pmData) {
            self.perModelSavedParameters = decodedPM
        }

        if let typoData = UserDefaults.standard.data(forKey: "MotifTypographyConfigV8"),
           let decodedTypo = try? JSONDecoder().decode(TypographyConfiguration.self, from: typoData) {
            self.typography = decodedTypo
        } else {
            self.typography = TypographyConfiguration()
        }

        if let shortData = UserDefaults.standard.data(forKey: "MotifShortcutsConfigV8"),
           let decodedShort = try? JSONDecoder().decode(ShortcutConfiguration.self, from: shortData) {
            self.shortcuts = decodedShort
        } else {
            self.shortcuts = ShortcutConfiguration()
        }

        let initialSnap = HardwareMemoryEngine.queryAppleSiliconMemory(
            liveOllamaTotalBytes: 0,
            estimatedKVCacheBytes: 0,
            baselineRAMBytesBeforeLoad: 0,
            baselineOllamaRSSBeforeLoad: 0,
            targetModelSizeBytes: 0,
            isLoadingWeights: false,
            sampleKernelProcessRSS: false,
            previousHistory: []
        )
        self.memorySnapshot = initialSnap
        self.baselineRAMBytesBeforeLoad = initialSnap.totalSystemUsedBytes

        supervisor.objectWillChange
            .receive(on: RunLoop.main)
            .sink { [weak self] _ in self?.objectWillChange.send() }
            .store(in: &cancellables)

        driveManager.objectWillChange
            .receive(on: RunLoop.main)
            .sink { [weak self] _ in self?.objectWillChange.send() }
            .store(in: &cancellables)

        loadSavedSessionsAndWorkspaces()
        if let notice = vault.lastRecoveryNotice {
            humanReadableErrorBanner = notice
        }
        setupAsyncFTS5SearchDebounce()
        installScopedTrackpadScrollMonitor()
        installKernelMemoryPressureMonitor()
        installMacOSLifecycleObservers()
        startAdaptiveTelemetryLoop()

        Task {
            self.recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
            await self.bootstrapLocalOllamaConnection()
        }
    }

    deinit {
        telemetryTask?.cancel()
        streamWatchdogTask?.cancel()
        streamingTask?.cancel()
        modelLoadTask?.cancel()
        searchTask?.cancel()
        memoryPressureSource?.cancel()
        if let monitor = scrollWheelMonitor { NSEvent.removeMonitor(monitor) }
        appObservers.forEach { NotificationCenter.default.removeObserver($0) }
        workspaceObservers.forEach { NSWorkspace.shared.notificationCenter.removeObserver($0) }
    }

    // MARK: - macOS Lifecycle ("Leave and Come Back", Sleep/Wake, Thermal, Quit)
    private func installMacOSLifecycleObservers() {
        let nc = NotificationCenter.default
        let wnc = NSWorkspace.shared.notificationCenter

        let enterFS = nc.addObserver(forName: NSWindow.didEnterFullScreenNotification, object: nil, queue: .main) { [weak self] _ in
            Task { @MainActor in
                self?.isWindowFullscreen = true
                self?.isScrolledAwayFromBottom = false
            }
        }
        let exitFS = nc.addObserver(forName: NSWindow.didExitFullScreenNotification, object: nil, queue: .main) { [weak self] _ in
            Task { @MainActor in
                self?.isWindowFullscreen = false
                self?.isScrolledAwayFromBottom = false
            }
        }
        let becomeActive = nc.addObserver(forName: NSApplication.didBecomeActiveNotification, object: nil, queue: .main) { [weak self] _ in
            Task { @MainActor in
                guard let self = self else { return }
                self.isAppActive = true
                await self.handleReturnToApp()
            }
        }
        let resignActive = nc.addObserver(forName: NSApplication.didResignActiveNotification, object: nil, queue: .main) { [weak self] _ in
            Task { @MainActor in
                self?.isAppActive = false
            }
        }
        let thermalObs = nc.addObserver(forName: ProcessInfo.thermalStateDidChangeNotification, object: nil, queue: .main) { [weak self] _ in
            Task { @MainActor in
                guard let self = self else { return }
                let state = ProcessInfo.processInfo.thermalState
                if state == .critical {
                    self.kernelMemoryPressureWarning = "Mac thermal state is critical — inference may throttle."
                }
            }
        }
        let quitObs = nc.addObserver(forName: NSApplication.willTerminateNotification, object: nil, queue: .main) { [weak self] _ in
            Task { @MainActor in
                guard let self = self else { return }
                self.saveCurrentDraftToActiveSession()
                self.saveAllState()
                if self.supervisor.autoUnloadOnQuit && !self.supervisor.isManagedByMotif {
                    for name in self.modelsLoadedByMotif {
                        await self.unloadModelByName(name)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        appObservers = [enterFS, exitFS, becomeActive, resignActive, thermalObs, quitObs]

        // Sleep & Wake MUST be observed on NSWorkspace.shared.notificationCenter!
        let sleepObs = wnc.addObserver(forName: NSWorkspace.willSleepNotification, object: nil, queue: .main) { [weak self] _ in
            Task { @MainActor in
                guard let self = self, self.supervisor.autoUnloadOnSleep, !self.isStreamingGeneration else { return }
                for name in self.modelsLoadedByMotif {
                    await self.unloadModelByName(name)
                }
            }
        }
        let wakeObs = wnc.addObserver(forName: NSWorkspace.didWakeNotification, object: nil, queue: .main) { [weak self] _ in
            Task { @MainActor in
                await self?.handleReturnToApp()
            }
        }
        workspaceObservers = [sleepObs, wakeObs]
    }

    /// "Leave and Come Back" handler: refreshes `/api/ps` and silently pre-warms the active chat's evicted model with exact `num_ctx`.
    private func handleReturnToApp() async {
        guard !isStreamingGeneration, !isLoadingModelWeights else { return }
        await pollOllamaRunningProcesses()
        recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)

        guard let session = activeSession,
              !session.activeBranchThread.isEmpty,
              let model = activeModelDescriptor,
              model.residency == .dormantOnDisk else { return }

        let estimatedTotal = model.sizeBytes + estimatedKVCacheBytes
        guard estimatedTotal < memorySnapshot.freeHeadroomBytes + liveOllamaTotalBytes else { return }

        subtleStatusPillMessage = "Warming \(model.shortDisplayName)…"
        await warmUpModelQuietly(modelName: model.name, parameters: session.parameters)
        await pollOllamaRunningProcesses()
        recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
        subtleStatusPillMessage = nil
    }

    // MARK: - Adaptive Battery-Friendly Telemetry Loop
    private func startAdaptiveTelemetryLoop() {
        telemetryTask?.cancel()
        telemetryTask = Task { [weak self] in
            while !Task.isCancelled {
                guard let self = self else { break }
                let shouldFastPoll = self.isLoadingModelWeights || self.isMemoryDetailPopoverPresented
                let shouldPollAtAll = self.isAppActive || self.isStreamingGeneration || self.isLoadingModelWeights

                if shouldPollAtAll && !self.isTelemetryQueryInFlight {
                    self.isTelemetryQueryInFlight = true
                    await self.pollOllamaRunningProcesses()
                    self.recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: shouldFastPoll)
                    self.isTelemetryQueryInFlight = false
                }

                let sleepNs: UInt64 = shouldFastPoll ? 500_000_000 : (shouldPollAtAll ? 5_000_000_000 : 12_000_000_000)
                try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: sleepNs)
            }
        }
    }

    private func installKernelMemoryPressureMonitor() {
        let source = DispatchSource.makeMemoryPressureSource(eventMask: [.warning, .critical], queue: .main)
        source.setEventHandler { [weak self] in
            guard let self = self else { return }
            let event = source.data
            Task { @MainActor in
                if event.contains(.critical) || event.contains(.warning) {
                    self.kernelMemoryPressureWarning = "macOS memory pressure is high (\(self.memorySnapshot.formattedHeadroom))."
                }
            }
        }
        source.resume()
        memoryPressureSource = source
    }

    private func setupAsyncFTS5SearchDebounce() {
        $sidebarSearchQuery
            .removeDuplicates()
            .debounce(for: .milliseconds(150), scheduler: RunLoop.main)
            .sink { [weak self] query in
                guard let self = self else { return }
                self.searchTask?.cancel()
                let trimmed = query.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
                guard trimmed.count >= 2 else {
                    self.ftsSearchResults = []
                    return
                }
                self.searchTask = Task {
                    let hits = await self.vault.searchMessagesFTS5Async(query: trimmed)
                    if !Task.isCancelled {
                        self.ftsSearchResults = hits
                    }
                }
            }
            .store(in: &cancellables)
    }

    /// Scoped scroll monitor: ignores scrolling inside the sidebar or right inspector!
    private func installScopedTrackpadScrollMonitor() {
        scrollWheelMonitor = NSEvent.addLocalMonitorForEvents(matching: .scrollWheel) { [weak self] event in
            guard let self = self else { return event }
            if event.scrollingDeltaY > 1.5, let win = event.window {
                let x = event.locationInWindow.x
                let leftBound: CGFloat = self.isSidebarVisible ? self.sidebarWidth : 0
                let rightBound: CGFloat = self.isRightUtilityPaneOpen ? (win.frame.width - 360) : win.frame.width
                if x > leftBound && x < rightBound {
                    Task { @MainActor in
                        if self.isStreamingGeneration && !self.isAutoScrollPaused {
                            self.isAutoScrollPaused = true
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            return event
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Active Session & Accurate KV Cache Math
    public var activeSession: ConversationSession? {
        guard !sessions.isEmpty, let id = selectedSessionID else { return nil }
        return sessions.first(where: { $0.id == id && !$0.isTrashed })
    }

    public var activeModelDescriptor: LocalModelDescriptor? {
        if let sessionModel = activeSession?.selectedModelName,
           !sessionModel.isEmpty,
           let matched = availableModels.first(where: { $0.name == sessionModel }) {
            return matched
        }
        return availableModels.first
    }

    public var estimatedKVCacheBytes: Int64 {
        let model = activeModelDescriptor
        let params = activeSession?.parameters ?? .nativeBalanced
        let effectiveCtx = model?.loadedContextLength ?? params.numCtx
        return HardwareMemoryEngine.estimateKVCacheBytes(
            numLayers: model?.numLayers ?? 32,
            numKVHeads: model?.numKVHeads ?? 8,
            headDim: model?.headDim ?? 128,
            numCtx: effectiveCtx,
            quant: params.kvCacheQuant,
            numParallel: max(1, supervisor.parallelRequestSlots)
        )
    }

    public func resetCustomPalette(for theme: AppThemeID) {
        switch theme {
        case .alabasterStudio: customPalettes.alabaster = .alabasterDefault
        case .warmEditorial: customPalettes.warmEditorial = .warmEditorialDefault
        default: break
        }
    }

    public func recordActiveModelParameterPreference() {
        guard !isApplyingProgrammaticModelSwitch,
              let session = activeSession,
              !session.selectedModelName.isEmpty else { return }
        perModelSavedParameters[session.selectedModelName] = session.parameters
        saveAllState()
    }

    public func preferredParameters(forModelName modelName: String) -> ModelRuntimeParameters {
        var params = perModelSavedParameters[modelName] ?? .nativeBalanced
        params.kvCacheQuant = supervisor.kvCacheTypeEnv
        if let desc = availableModels.first(where: { $0.name == modelName }) {
            let valid = desc.thinkingProfile.validOptions
            if !valid.contains(params.thinkingMode) {
                params.thinkingMode = desc.thinkingProfile.preferredDefaultOption
            }
            if params.numCtx > desc.maxContextLength {
                params.numCtx = desc.maxContextLength
            }
        }
        return params
    }

    // MARK: - Slash Personas
    public var matchedSlashPersonas: [SlashPersona] {
        guard !isSlashMenuDismissedForCurrentToken else { return [] }
        let trimmed = composerDraft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        guard trimmed.hasPrefix("/") else { return [] }
        let firstLine = trimmed.components(separatedBy: .newlines).first ?? trimmed
        let tokens = firstLine.components(separatedBy: .whitespaces).filter { !$0.isEmpty }
        guard let firstToken = tokens.first?.lowercased(), tokens.count <= 2 else { return [] }
        if firstToken == "/" { return slashPersonas }
        return slashPersonas.filter {
            $0.trigger.lowercased().hasPrefix(firstToken) || $0.title.lowercased().contains(firstToken.dropFirst())
        }
    }

    public func moveSlashSelection(delta: Int) {
        let list = matchedSlashPersonas
        guard !list.isEmpty else { return }
        slashMenuHighlightedIndex = (slashMenuHighlightedIndex + delta + list.count) % list.count
    }

    public func applyHighlightedSlashPersona() -> Bool {
        let list = matchedSlashPersonas
        guard !list.isEmpty else { return false }
        applySlashPersona(list[min(max(0, slashMenuHighlightedIndex), list.count - 1)])
        return true
    }

    public func applySlashPersona(_ persona: SlashPersona) {
        guard let idx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == selectedSessionID }) else { return }
        sessions[idx].systemPrompt = persona.systemPrompt
        sessions[idx].activePersonaTrigger = persona.trigger
        let trimmed = composerDraft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        if let firstSpace = trimmed.firstIndex(where: { $0.isWhitespace }) {
            composerDraft = String(trimmed[firstSpace...]).trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        } else {
            composerDraft = ""
        }
        sessions[idx].draftText = composerDraft
        slashMenuHighlightedIndex = 0
        isSlashMenuDismissedForCurrentToken = false
        saveAllState()
    }

    // MARK: - Ollama Discovery (`/api/tags`), Introspection (`/api/show`), Residency (`/api/ps`)
    public func bootstrapLocalOllamaConnection() async {
        let extPath = driveManager.resolvedModelsPath
        if await supervisor.probeVersion() {
            await refreshLocalEngineModels()
        } else {
            await supervisor.launchManagedDaemonIfNeeded(externalModelsDir: extPath)
            await refreshLocalEngineModels()
        }
        await pollOllamaRunningProcesses()
        recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
    }

    public func selectExternalDriveAndReloadModels() {
        if driveManager.selectExternalDriveDirectory() {
            let newPath = driveManager.resolvedModelsPath
            Task {
                if supervisor.isManagedByMotif {
                    _ = await supervisor.restartManagedDaemonOnly(modelsPath: newPath)
                    await refreshLocalEngineModels()
                    await pollOllamaRunningProcesses()
                } else {
                    humanReadableErrorBanner = "External Ollama detected. Restart your external Ollama server with OLLAMA_MODELS=\"\(newPath)\" or switch to Motif-Managed mode in Settings."
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public func applyDaemonConfigurationAndRestart() {
        if downloadTasks.contains(where: { !$0.isCompleted }) {
            humanReadableErrorBanner = "Wait for active model downloads to finish before restarting Ollama."
            return
        }
        guard supervisor.isManagedByMotif else {
            humanReadableErrorBanner = "Attached to an external Ollama daemon. Set OLLAMA_FLASH_ATTENTION=1 and OLLAMA_KV_CACHE_TYPE=\(supervisor.kvCacheTypeEnv.rawValue) in your external daemon environment, then restart it."
            return
        }
        let extPath = driveManager.resolvedModelsPath
        let modelToReload = availableModels.first(where: { $0.residency == .activeInUnifiedRAM })
        Task {
            _ = await supervisor.restartManagedDaemonOnly(modelsPath: extPath)
            await refreshLocalEngineModels()
            if let model = modelToReload {
                await loadModelWithLiveRAMDelta(model)
            } else {
                await pollOllamaRunningProcesses()
                recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
            }
        }
    }

    public func applyKVCacheQuantizationLive(_ newQuant: KVCacheQuantization) {
        guard !isApplyingProgrammaticModelSwitch else { return }
        guard supervisor.kvCacheTypeEnv != newQuant else {
            recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
            return
        }
        supervisor.kvCacheTypeEnv = newQuant
        if newQuant != .f16 { supervisor.enableFlashAttentionEnv = true }
        recordActiveModelParameterPreference()
        recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
        if supervisor.isManagedByMotif {
            applyDaemonConfigurationAndRestart()
        } else {
            humanReadableErrorBanner = "KV Cache preference updated to \(newQuant.rawValue.uppercased()). Because Ollama is running externally, restart it with OLLAMA_FLASH_ATTENTION=1 OLLAMA_KV_CACHE_TYPE=\(newQuant.rawValue) for this to take effect."
        }
    }

    public func applyTightRAMOptimization(andLoadModel targetModel: LocalModelDescriptor? = nil) {
        guard let sIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == selectedSessionID }) else { return }
        let model = targetModel ?? activeModelDescriptor

        var safeGPULayers = -1
        if let m = model {
            let totalLayers = max(1, m.numLayers)
            let safeBudgetBytes = max(
                memorySnapshot.freeHeadroomBytes + liveOllamaTotalBytes - 750_000_000,
                memorySnapshot.metalRecommendedMaxBytes - 1_073_741_824
            )
            if m.sizeBytes > safeBudgetBytes && m.sizeBytes > 0 {
                let ratio = max(0.25, min(0.95, Double(safeBudgetBytes) / Double(m.sizeBytes)))
                safeGPULayers = max(4, Int(Double(totalLayers) * ratio))
            }
        }

        sessions[sIdx].parameters.tightRAMModeEnabled = true
        sessions[sIdx].parameters.kvCacheQuant = .q4_0
        sessions[sIdx].parameters.numCtx = min(sessions[sIdx].parameters.numCtx, 4096)
        sessions[sIdx].parameters.numBatch = 128
        sessions[sIdx].parameters.numKeep = 128
        sessions[sIdx].parameters.numGpu = safeGPULayers
        sessions[sIdx].parameters.numThread = 0
        sessions[sIdx].parameters.useMmap = true
        sessions[sIdx].parameters.useMlock = false
        sessions[sIdx].parameters.keepAliveMinutes = 5
        if let m = model {
            sessions[sIdx].selectedModelName = m.name
            perModelSavedParameters[m.name] = sessions[sIdx].parameters
        }
        saveAllState()

        let needsDaemonRestart = supervisor.isManagedByMotif
            && (supervisor.kvCacheTypeEnv != .q4_0 || !supervisor.enableFlashAttentionEnv || supervisor.parallelRequestSlots != 1)
        supervisor.kvCacheTypeEnv = .q4_0
        supervisor.enableFlashAttentionEnv = true
        supervisor.parallelRequestSlots = 1
        supervisor.maxLoadedModels = 1
        kernelMemoryPressureWarning = nil

        Task {
            if needsDaemonRestart {
                _ = await supervisor.restartManagedDaemonOnly(modelsPath: driveManager.resolvedModelsPath)
                await refreshLocalEngineModels()
            }
            if let m = model {
                await loadModelWithLiveRAMDelta(m)
            } else {
                recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
            }
        }
    }

    public func refreshLocalEngineModels() async {
        guard let url = URL(string: "\(supervisor.normalizedEndpoint)/api/tags") else { return }
        var req = URLRequest(url: url)
        req.timeoutInterval = 5.0

        do {
            let (data, response) = try await OllamaNetworking.ephemeralSession.data(for: req)
            guard let http = response as? HTTPURLResponse, http.statusCode == 200,
                  let json = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any],
                  let modelsArr = json["models"] as? [[String: Any]] else { return }

            supervisor.isEngineOnline = true
            let modeTag = supervisor.isManagedByMotif ? "Managed" : "External"
            supervisor.supervisorStatusLog = "\(modeTag) Ollama \(supervisor.ollamaVersionString) (\(modelsArr.count) \(modelsArr.count == 1 ? "model" : "models"))."

            var parsed: [LocalModelDescriptor] = []
            for item in modelsArr {
                let name = (item["name"] as? String) ?? ""
                guard !name.isEmpty else { continue }
                let size = (item["size"] as? NSNumber)?.int64Value ?? 0
                let details = item["details"] as? [String: Any]
                let family = (details?["family"] as? String) ?? "model"
                let paramSize = (details?["parameter_size"] as? String) ?? ""
                let quant = (details?["quantization_level"] as? String) ?? ""

                let existing = availableModels.first(where: { $0.name == name })
                parsed.append(LocalModelDescriptor(
                    name: name,
                    displayName: LocalModelDescriptor.shortenModelName(name),
                    family: family,
                    parameterSize: paramSize,
                    quantizationLevel: quant,
                    sizeBytes: size,
                    digest: (item["digest"] as? String) ?? "",
                    modifiedAt: Date(),
                    residency: existing?.residency ?? .dormantOnDisk,
                    totalLoadedSizeBytes: existing?.totalLoadedSizeBytes ?? 0,
                    vramAllocatedBytes: existing?.vramAllocatedBytes ?? 0,
                    numLayers: existing?.numLayers ?? 32,
                    numKVHeads: existing?.numKVHeads ?? 8,
                    headDim: existing?.headDim ?? 128,
                    maxContextLength: existing?.maxContextLength ?? 32768,
                    supportsVision: existing?.supportsVision ?? false,
                    supportsTools: existing?.supportsTools ?? false,
                    thinkingProfile: existing?.thinkingProfile ?? Self.heuristicThinkingProfile(forModelName: name),
                    hasIntrospectedShowMetadata: existing?.hasIntrospectedShowMetadata ?? false,
                    residencyExpiresAt: existing?.residencyExpiresAt,
                    loadedContextLength: existing?.loadedContextLength
                ))
            }

            self.availableModels = parsed

            // Gracefully handle deleted models without losing historical per-message attribution
            if let firstModel = parsed.first {
                for idx in sessions.indices {
                    if sessions[idx].selectedModelName.isEmpty || !parsed.contains(where: { $0.name == sessions[idx].selectedModelName }) {
                        sessions[idx].selectedModelName = firstModel.name
                        if sessions[idx].activeBranchThread.isEmpty {
                            sessions[idx].parameters = preferredParameters(forModelName: firstModel.name)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            for model in parsed where !model.hasIntrospectedShowMetadata {
                await introspectModelCapabilitiesViaShowAPI(modelName: model.name)
            }
        } catch {
            supervisor.isEngineOnline = false
            supervisor.supervisorStatusLog = "Ollama daemon offline at \(supervisor.normalizedEndpoint)."
        }
    }

    public func introspectModelCapabilitiesViaShowAPI(modelName: String) async {
        guard let url = URL(string: "\(supervisor.normalizedEndpoint)/api/show") else { return }
        var req = URLRequest(url: url)
        req.httpMethod = "POST"
        req.setValue("application/json", forHTTPHeaderField: "Content-Type")
        req.timeoutInterval = 30.0
        req.httpBody = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: ["model": modelName])

        guard let (data, response) = try? await OllamaNetworking.ephemeralSession.data(for: req),
              let http = response as? HTTPURLResponse, http.statusCode == 200,
              let json = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any] else { return }

        let template = (json["template"] as? String) ?? ""
        let capabilities = (json["capabilities"] as? [String]) ?? []
        let modelInfo = (json["model_info"] as? [String: Any]) ?? [:]

        let supportsVision = capabilities.contains("vision") || modelInfo.keys.contains(where: { $0.contains(".vision.") })
        let supportsTools = capabilities.contains("tools")
        let hasThinkingCap = capabilities.contains("thinking")
        let templateHasThinkVar = template.contains(".Thinking") || template.contains("<think>") || template.contains("<|thought|>")
        let templateHasSlashNoThink = template.contains("/no_think") || template.contains("/think")
        let templateHasEffort = template.contains("reasoning_effort") || template.contains("thinking_effort")

        var detectedTokensList: [String] = []
        if hasThinkingCap { detectedTokensList.append("capabilities: [thinking]") }
        if supportsVision { detectedTokensList.append("vision") }
        if supportsTools { detectedTokensList.append("tools") }
        if template.contains(".Thinking") { detectedTokensList.append("{{ .Thinking }}") }
        if template.contains("<think>") { detectedTokensList.append("<think>…</think>") }
        if templateHasSlashNoThink { detectedTokensList.append("/think · /no_think") }

        var profile: ModelThinkingProfile
        if let thinkingObj = json["thinking"] as? [String: Any],
           let rawValues = thinkingObj["values"] as? [Any] {
            let stringLevels = rawValues.compactMap { $0 as? String }
            let boolValues = rawValues.compactMap { $0 as? Bool }
            let allowsDisable = boolValues.contains(false)

            var defaultOpt: ThinkingModeOption? = nil
            if let defStr = thinkingObj["default"] as? String {
                defaultOpt = .level(defStr)
            } else if let defBool = thinkingObj["default"] as? Bool {
                defaultOpt = defBool ? .on : .off
            }

            if !stringLevels.isEmpty {
                detectedTokensList.insert("thinking.values: [\(stringLevels.joined(separator: " · "))]", at: 0)
                profile = ModelThinkingProfile(
                    kind: .effortTiered,
                    supportsSlashDirectives: templateHasSlashNoThink,
                    supportsNativeThinkFlag: true,
                    supportsEffortLevels: true,
                    allowsDisable: allowsDisable,
                    serverDeclaredLevels: stringLevels,
                    serverDefaultOption: defaultOpt,
                    detectedMechanismSummary: "Ollama levels: \(stringLevels.joined(separator: " / "))",
                    detectedTemplateTokens: detectedTokensList.joined(separator: " | ")
                )
            } else if boolValues.contains(true) {
                detectedTokensList.insert("thinking.values: [off/on]", at: 0)
                profile = ModelThinkingProfile(
                    kind: allowsDisable ? .toggleable : .alwaysOnReasoning,
                    supportsSlashDirectives: templateHasSlashNoThink,
                    supportsNativeThinkFlag: true,
                    supportsEffortLevels: false,
                    allowsDisable: allowsDisable,
                    serverDeclaredLevels: nil,
                    serverDefaultOption: defaultOpt,
                    detectedMechanismSummary: allowsDisable ? "Native Ollama binary thinking" : "Always-on reasoning model",
                    detectedTemplateTokens: detectedTokensList.joined(separator: " | ")
                )
            } else {
                // Authoritative [false]: explicitly NO thinking support!
                profile = ModelThinkingProfile(
                    kind: .unsupported,
                    supportsSlashDirectives: false,
                    supportsNativeThinkFlag: false,
                    supportsEffortLevels: false,
                    allowsDisable: true,
                    serverDeclaredLevels: nil,
                    serverDefaultOption: .off,
                    detectedMechanismSummary: "Standard non-reasoning model (declared by /api/show)",
                    detectedTemplateTokens: detectedTokensList.isEmpty ? "None" : detectedTokensList.joined(separator: " | ")
                )
            }
        } else if templateHasEffort {
            profile = ModelThinkingProfile(
                kind: .effortTiered,
                supportsSlashDirectives: templateHasSlashNoThink,
                supportsNativeThinkFlag: true,
                supportsEffortLevels: true,
                allowsDisable: true,
                serverDeclaredLevels: ["low", "medium", "high"],
                serverDefaultOption: .level("medium"),
                detectedMechanismSummary: "Template supports multi-level reasoning effort",
                detectedTemplateTokens: detectedTokensList.joined(separator: " | ")
            )
        } else if hasThinkingCap || templateHasThinkVar || templateHasSlashNoThink {
            profile = ModelThinkingProfile(
                kind: .toggleable,
                supportsSlashDirectives: templateHasSlashNoThink,
                supportsNativeThinkFlag: true,
                supportsEffortLevels: false,
                allowsDisable: true,
                serverDeclaredLevels: nil,
                serverDefaultOption: .on,
                detectedMechanismSummary: "Supports native Ollama thinking channel",
                detectedTemplateTokens: detectedTokensList.joined(separator: " | ")
            )
        } else {
            profile = Self.heuristicThinkingProfile(forModelName: modelName)
        }

        // Extract exact architecture dimensions for accurate KV cache math
        var detectedLayers = 32
        var detectedKVHeads = 8
        var detectedHeadDim = 128
        var detectedCtx = 32768
        for (key, val) in modelInfo {
            let intVal = (val as? NSNumber)?.intValue
            guard let v = intVal, v > 0 else { continue }
            if key.hasSuffix(".block_count") { detectedLayers = v }
            else if key.hasSuffix(".attention.head_count_kv") { detectedKVHeads = v }
            else if key.hasSuffix(".attention.key_length") || key.hasSuffix(".attention.value_length") { detectedHeadDim = v }
            else if key.hasSuffix(".context_length") { detectedCtx = v }
        }

        if let idx = availableModels.firstIndex(where: { $0.name == modelName }) {
            availableModels[idx].thinkingProfile = profile
            availableModels[idx].numLayers = detectedLayers
            availableModels[idx].numKVHeads = detectedKVHeads
            availableModels[idx].headDim = detectedHeadDim
            availableModels[idx].maxContextLength = detectedCtx
            availableModels[idx].supportsVision = supportsVision
            availableModels[idx].supportsTools = supportsTools
            availableModels[idx].hasIntrospectedShowMetadata = true

            for sIdx in sessions.indices where sessions[sIdx].selectedModelName == modelName {
                let valid = profile.validOptions
                if let savedPref = perModelSavedParameters[modelName], valid.contains(savedPref.thinkingMode) {
                    if sessions[sIdx].activeBranchThread.isEmpty {
                        sessions[sIdx].parameters = savedPref
                    }
                } else if !valid.contains(sessions[sIdx].parameters.thinkingMode) {
                    sessions[sIdx].parameters.thinkingMode = profile.preferredDefaultOption
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private static func heuristicThinkingProfile(forModelName name: String) -> ModelThinkingProfile {
        let lower = name.lowercased()
        if lower.contains("gpt-oss") || lower.contains("o1") || lower.contains("o3") {
            return ModelThinkingProfile(
                kind: .effortTiered, supportsNativeThinkFlag: true, supportsEffortLevels: true,
                serverDeclaredLevels: ["low", "medium", "high"], serverDefaultOption: .level("medium"),
                detectedMechanismSummary: "Supports Off / Low / Med / High reasoning effort",
                detectedTemplateTokens: "effort: low · medium · high"
            )
        } else if lower.contains("qwen3") || lower.contains("r1") || lower.contains("qwq") || lower.contains("think") || lower.contains("reasoning") {
            return ModelThinkingProfile(
                kind: .toggleable, supportsNativeThinkFlag: true, serverDefaultOption: .on,
                detectedMechanismSummary: "Supports native deliberation",
                detectedTemplateTokens: "capabilities: [thinking]"
            )
        }
        return ModelThinkingProfile()
    }

    public func pollOllamaRunningProcesses() async {
        guard let url = URL(string: "\(supervisor.normalizedEndpoint)/api/ps") else { return }
        var req = URLRequest(url: url)
        req.timeoutInterval = 2.0

        do {
            let (data, response) = try await OllamaNetworking.ephemeralSession.data(for: req)
            guard let http = response as? HTTPURLResponse, http.statusCode == 200,
                  let json = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any],
                  let runningArr = json["models"] as? [[String: Any]] else { return }

            supervisor.isEngineOnline = true
            var totalResident: Int64 = 0
            var totalMap: [String: Int64] = [:]
            var vramMap: [String: Int64] = [:]
            var expiryMap: [String: Date] = [:]
            var loadedCtxMap: [String: Int] = [:]

            for item in runningArr {
                let name = (item["name"] as? String) ?? ""
                guard !name.isEmpty else { continue }
                let sizeTotal = (item["size"] as? NSNumber)?.int64Value ?? 0
                let sizeVRAM = (item["size_vram"] as? NSNumber)?.int64Value ?? sizeTotal
                totalMap[name] = max(sizeTotal, sizeVRAM)
                vramMap[name] = sizeVRAM
                totalResident += max(sizeTotal, sizeVRAM)
                if let expISO = item["expires_at"] as? String,
                   let parsedDate = OllamaNetworking.parseRFC3339Date(expISO) {
                    expiryMap[name] = parsedDate
                }
                if let loadedCtx = (item["context_length"] as? NSNumber)?.intValue, loadedCtx > 0 {
                    loadedCtxMap[name] = loadedCtx
                }
            }

            for idx in availableModels.indices {
                let modelName = availableModels[idx].name
                if let tot = totalMap[modelName] {
                    availableModels[idx].residency = .activeInUnifiedRAM
                    availableModels[idx].totalLoadedSizeBytes = tot
                    availableModels[idx].vramAllocatedBytes = vramMap[modelName] ?? tot
                    availableModels[idx].residencyExpiresAt = expiryMap[modelName]
                    availableModels[idx].loadedContextLength = loadedCtxMap[modelName]
                } else if isLoadingModelWeights && loadingModelInternalID == modelName {
                    availableModels[idx].residency = .loadingIntoMemory
                } else {
                    availableModels[idx].residency = .dormantOnDisk
                    availableModels[idx].totalLoadedSizeBytes = 0
                    availableModels[idx].vramAllocatedBytes = 0
                    availableModels[idx].residencyExpiresAt = nil
                    availableModels[idx].loadedContextLength = nil
                }
            }
            self.liveOllamaTotalBytes = totalResident
        } catch {}
    }

    public func recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: Bool) {
        let targetBytes = isLoadingModelWeights ? targetLoadingModelBytes : (activeModelDescriptor?.sizeBytes ?? 0)
        let isLoading = isLoadingModelWeights
        let reportedTotal = liveOllamaTotalBytes
        let baseOllamaRSS = baselineOllamaRSSBeforeLoad
        let kvBytes = estimatedKVCacheBytes
        let baseBytes = baselineRAMBytesBeforeLoad
        let prevHist = memorySnapshot.historySamplesGB

        Task.detached(priority: .utility) {
            let snap = HardwareMemoryEngine.queryAppleSiliconMemory(
                liveOllamaTotalBytes: reportedTotal,
                estimatedKVCacheBytes: kvBytes,
                baselineRAMBytesBeforeLoad: baseBytes,
                baselineOllamaRSSBeforeLoad: baseOllamaRSS,
                targetModelSizeBytes: targetBytes,
                isLoadingWeights: isLoading,
                sampleKernelProcessRSS: sampleKernelRSS,
                previousHistory: prevHist
            )
            await MainActor.run { [weak self] in
                self?.memorySnapshot = snap
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Model Selection & Matched-Options Warm-Up (Fixes Turn-1 Double Reload!)
    public func selectModelOnly(_ targetModel: LocalModelDescriptor) {
        guard let sIdx = sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == selectedSessionID }) else { return }
        isApplyingProgrammaticModelSwitch = true
        sessions[sIdx].selectedModelName = targetModel.name

        if let savedParams = perModelSavedParameters[targetModel.name] {
            var restored = savedParams
            restored.kvCacheQuant = supervisor.kvCacheTypeEnv
            let validModes = targetModel.thinkingProfile.validOptions
            if !validModes.contains(restored.thinkingMode) {
                restored.thinkingMode = targetModel.thinkingProfile.preferredDefaultOption
            }
            sessions[sIdx].parameters = restored
        } else {
