public struct MemoryDetailPopoverView: View {
    @ObservedObject public var store: WorkstationStore
    public let tokens: MotifTokens

    private var snap: UnifiedMemorySnapshot { store.memorySnapshot }
    private var typo: TypographyConfiguration { store.typography }

    private func formattedCountdown(until expiry: Date?) -> String? {
        guard let expiry = expiry else { return nil }
        let secs = Int(expiry.timeIntervalSinceNow)
        if secs <= 0 { return "Evicting soon" }
        if secs > 86400 * 30 { return "Pinned" }
        let mins = secs / 60
        let rem = secs % 60
        return mins > 0 ? "\(mins)m \(rem)s" : "\(rem)s"
    }

    public var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            HStack {
                Text("Unified Memory & Residency")
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12.5, weight: .semibold))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                Spacer()
                Text(String(format: "%.1f GB free", snap.headroomGB))
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold).monospacedDigit())
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.accentAction)
            }

            GeometryReader { geo in
                let totalW = geo.size.width
                let wWeights = max(0, CGFloat(snap.weightsFraction) * totalW)
                let wTotal = max(6, CGFloat(snap.usageFraction) * totalW)
                ZStack(alignment: .leading) {
                    Capsule().fill(tokens.elevatedSurface).frame(height: 6)
                    Capsule().fill(tokens.secondaryText.opacity(0.45)).frame(width: wTotal, height: 6)
                    if wWeights > 0 {
                        Capsule().fill(tokens.accentAction).frame(width: wWeights, height: 6)
                    }
                }
            }
            .frame(height: 6)

            if snap.historySamplesGB.count > 2 {
                GeometryReader { geo in
                    let maxVal = max(snap.totalGB, 8.0)
                    Path { path in
                        for (idx, val) in snap.historySamplesGB.enumerated() {
                            let x = geo.size.width * CGFloat(idx) / CGFloat(max(1, snap.historySamplesGB.count - 1))
                            let y = geo.size.height * (1.0 - CGFloat(min(1.0, val / maxVal)))
                            if idx == 0 { path.move(to: CGPoint(x: x, y: y)) }
                            else { path.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: x, y: y)) }
                        }
                    }
                    .stroke(tokens.accentAction, lineWidth: 1.4)
                }
                .frame(height: 22)
            }

            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

            VStack(spacing: 6) {
                memoryRow("Free / Remaining RAM", String(format: "%.2f GB", snap.headroomGB))
                memoryRow("Total System RAM Used", String(format: "%.2f / %.0f GB", snap.usedGB, snap.totalGB))
                memoryRow("Ollama Resident (Weights)", String(format: "%.2f GB", snap.weightsGB))
                memoryRow("Estimated KV Cache (\(store.supervisor.kvCacheTypeEnv.rawValue.uppercased()))", String(format: "%.2f GB", snap.kvCacheGB))
                memoryRow("Metal Recommended Ceiling", String(format: "%.1f GB", snap.metalMaxGB))
            }

            let residentModels = store.availableModels.filter { $0.residency == .activeInUnifiedRAM }
            if !residentModels.isEmpty {
                HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                    Text("LOADED IN MEMORY (/api/ps)")
                        .font(typo.mono(size: 9.5, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)

                    ForEach(residentModels) { model in
                        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                            HStack {
                                Text(model.shortDisplayName)
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                Spacer()
                                Button("Unload") {
                                    Task { await store.unloadModelFromMemory(model) }
                                }
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5, weight: .medium))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                            }

                            HStack(spacing: 8) {
                                if let gpuFrac = model.gpuOffloadFraction {
                                    let gpuPct = Int(round(gpuFrac * 100.0))
                                    Text(gpuPct >= 98 ? "100% Metal GPU" : "\(gpuPct)% GPU / \(100 - gpuPct)% CPU")
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                                        .foregroundColor(model.hasCPUSpillover ? tokens.statusWarning : tokens.statusOnline)
                                }
                                if let ctx = model.loadedContextLength {
                                    Text("· \(ctx) ctx")
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5).monospacedDigit())
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                }
                                if let cd = formattedCountdown(until: model.residencyExpiresAt) {
                                    Spacer()
                                    Text("TTL \(cd)")
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5).monospacedDigit())
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        .padding(8)
                        .background(tokens.elevatedSurface.opacity(0.6))
                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                    }
                }
            }

            if let session = store.activeSession {
                HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)
                let usedTok = session.estimatedActiveTokens
                let maxTok = max(1, session.parameters.numCtx)
                let pct = Int(min(100.0, (Double(usedTok) / Double(maxTok)) * 100.0))
                let thread = session.activeBranchThread

                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                    HStack {
                        Text("Active Chat Context")
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                        Spacer()
                        Text("\(usedTok) / \(maxTok) tok (\(pct)%)")
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                            .foregroundColor(pct >= 80 ? tokens.statusWarning : tokens.secondaryText)
                    }

                    HStack(spacing: 8) {
                        Button {
                            store.trimOldestTurnInActiveSession()
                        } label: {
                            HStack(spacing: 4) {
                                Image(systemName: "scissors").font(.system(size: 10, weight: .semibold))
                                Text("Trim Oldest Turn").font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                            }
                            .foregroundColor(thread.isEmpty ? tokens.mutedText : tokens.primaryText)
                            .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 5)
                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                            .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)
                            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                        }
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        .disabled(thread.isEmpty)

                        Button {
                            store.isMemoryDetailPopoverPresented = false
                            store.summarizeContextIntoNewBranch()
                        } label: {
                            HStack(spacing: 4) {
                                Image(systemName: "arrow.triangle.branch").font(.system(size: 10, weight: .semibold))
                                Text("Summarize Chat").font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                            }
                            .foregroundColor(thread.isEmpty ? tokens.mutedText : tokens.primaryText)
                            .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 5)
                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                            .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)
                            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                        }
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        .disabled(thread.isEmpty)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        .padding(14)
        .frame(width: 360)
        .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
    }

    private func memoryRow(_ label: String, _ value: String) -> some View {
        HStack {
            Text(label).font(typo.sans(size: 11.5)).foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
            Spacer()
            Text(value).font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit()).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
        }
    }

public struct HighContrastJumpToLatestButton: View {
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    public let action: () -> Void
    @State private var isHovered: Bool = false

    private var buttonSurfaceColor: Color {
        if tokens.theme == .alabasterStudio && !tokens.hasCustomAccent {
            return Color(red: 0.239, green: 0.259, blue: 0.302).opacity(isHovered ? 0.96 : 0.88)
        }
        return tokens.accentAction.opacity(isHovered ? 0.98 : 0.92)
    }

    private var arrowForegroundColor: Color {
        if tokens.theme == .alabasterStudio && !tokens.hasCustomAccent { return Color.white }
        return tokens.accentForeground
    }

    public var body: some View {
        Button(action: action) {
            ZStack {
                Circle().fill(buttonSurfaceColor).frame(width: 34, height: 34)
                Image(systemName: "arrow.down")
                    .font(.system(size: 12.0, weight: .bold))
                    .foregroundColor(arrowForegroundColor)
            }
            .overlay(Circle().strokeBorder(tokens.strongBorder.opacity(0.45), lineWidth: 1))
            .shadow(color: Color.black.opacity(tokens.isEffectivelyDark ? 0.35 : 0.14), radius: 6, y: 2)
            .scaleEffect(isHovered ? 1.06 : 1.0)
        }
        .buttonStyle(.plain)
        .onHover { hovering in withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.14)) { isHovered = hovering } }
        .help("Scroll to bottom")
    }
}

// MARK: - Currency-Safe LaTeX Normalizer (Never Mangles "$5 to $10"!)
public enum LaTeXAndSymbolNormalizer {
    private static let mathHintChars = CharacterSet(charactersIn: "\\^_=+*/<>±×÷≈≠≤≥∑∫√αβγδεθλμπσωΔΣΩ")

    public static func normalizeProse(_ input: String) -> String {
        guard input.contains("$") || input.contains("\\") else { return input }
        var s = input

        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "$$", with: "")
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\[", with: "")
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\]", with: "")
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\(", with: "")
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\)", with: "")

        let wrappers = [
            "\\\\textbf\\{([^\\}]*)\\}": "**$1**",
            "\\\\textit\\{([^\\}]*)\\}": "*$1*",
            "\\\\text\\{([^\\}]*)\\}": "$1",
            "\\\\mathrm\\{([^\\}]*)\\}": "$1",
            "\\\\mathbf\\{([^\\}]*)\\}": "**$1**",
            "\\\\sqrt\\{([^\\}]*)\\}": "√($1)",
            "\\\\frac\\{([^\\}]*)\\}\\{([^\\}]*)\\}": "($1/$2)"
        ]
        for (pattern, template) in wrappers {
            if let regex = try? NSRegularExpression(pattern: pattern) {
                s = regex.stringByReplacingMatches(in: s, range: NSRange(s.startIndex..., in: s), withTemplate: template)
            }
        }

        // Only unwrap $...$ if it genuinely looks like math, NOT currency like "$5 to $10"!
        if s.contains("$"), let inlineMathRegex = try? NSRegularExpression(pattern: "\\$(?!\\s)([^$\\n]+?)(?<!\\s)\\$") {
            let ns = s as NSString
            let matches = inlineMathRegex.matches(in: s, range: NSRange(location: 0, length: ns.length))
            if !matches.isEmpty {
                var rebuilt = s
                for match in matches.reversed() where match.numberOfRanges >= 2 {
                    guard let fullRange = Range(match.range(at: 0), in: rebuilt),
                          let innerRange = Range(match.range(at: 1), in: rebuilt) else { continue }
                    let rawInner = String(rebuilt[innerRange])

                    // Reject currency spans (e.g., "5 to ", "12.50 - ", or starting with digit + space)
                    let startsWithNumberAndSpace = rawInner.first?.isNumber == true && rawInner.contains(" ")
                    let hasMathHint = rawInner.unicodeScalars.contains(where: { mathHintChars.contains($0) })
                    let isSingleVariable = rawInner.count <= 2 && rawInner.allSatisfy({ $0.isLetter })
                    if startsWithNumberAndSpace || (!hasMathHint && !isSingleVariable) {
                        continue
                    }

                    var mathInner = rawInner
                    let mathSubSup: [(String, String)] = [
                        ("^2", "²"), ("^3", "³"), ("^n", "ⁿ"),
                        ("_0", "₀"), ("_1", "₁"), ("_2", "₂"), ("_i", "ᵢ"), ("_n", "ₙ")
                    ]
                    for (k, v) in mathSubSup { mathInner = mathInner.replacingOccurrences(of: k, with: v) }
                    rebuilt.replaceSubrange(fullRange, with: mathInner)
                }
                s = rebuilt
            }
        }

        let replacements: [(String, String)] = [
            ("\\times", "×"), ("\\div", "÷"), ("\\pm", "±"), ("\\mp", "∓"),
            ("\\cdot", "·"), ("\\approx", "≈"), ("\\neq", "≠"), ("\\ne", "≠"),
            ("\\leq", "≤"), ("\\le", "≤"), ("\\geq", "≥"), ("\\ge", "≥"),
            ("\\rightarrow", "→"), ("\\to", "→"), ("\\leftarrow", "←"),
            ("\\Rightarrow", "⇒"), ("\\Leftarrow", "⇐"), ("\\leftrightarrow", "↔"),
            ("\\infty", "∞"), ("\\partial", "∂"), ("\\nabla", "∇"),
            ("\\forall", "∀"), ("\\exists", "∃"), ("\\in", "∈"), ("\\notin", "∉"),
            ("\\subset", "⊂"), ("\\subseteq", "⊆"), ("\\cup", "∪"), ("\\cap", "∩"),
            ("\\sum", "∑"), ("\\prod", "∏"), ("\\int", "∫"),
            ("\\alpha", "α"), ("\\beta", "β"), ("\\gamma", "γ"), ("\\delta", "δ"),
            ("\\epsilon", "ε"), ("\\zeta", "ζ"), ("\\eta", "η"), ("\\theta", "θ"),
            ("\\lambda", "λ"), ("\\mu", "μ"), ("\\pi", "π"), ("\\rho", "ρ"),
            ("\\sigma", "σ"), ("\\tau", "τ"), ("\\phi", "φ"), ("\\chi", "χ"),
            ("\\psi", "ψ"), ("\\omega", "ω"), ("\\Delta", "Δ"), ("\\Sigma", "Σ"),
            ("\\Omega", "Ω"), ("\\Gamma", "Γ"), ("\\Lambda", "Λ"), ("\\Theta", "Θ")
        ]
        for (latex, unicode) in replacements { s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: latex, with: unicode) }
        return s
    }
}

// MARK: - Markdown Block AST (With Tables, Nested Lists, Exact-Key Cache & Streaming Prefix Freezing)
public enum MarkdownBlockKind: Equatable, Sendable {
    case paragraph(String)
    case heading(Int, String)
    case bulletItem(Int, String)
    case numberedItem(Int, String, String)
    case blockquote(String)
    case horizontalRule
    case codeBlock(String, String)
    case table([String], [[String]])
}

public struct MarkdownBlock: Identifiable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public let id: String
    public let kind: MarkdownBlockKind
}

@MainActor
public final class MarkdownParseCache {
    public static let shared = MarkdownParseCache()
    private var exactCache: [String: [MarkdownBlock]] = [:]

    public func blocks(for raw: String, isStreaming: Bool, builder: () -> [MarkdownBlock]) -> [MarkdownBlock] {
        if !isStreaming, let cached = exactCache[raw] {
            return cached
        }
        let computed = builder()
        if !isStreaming {
            if exactCache.count > 250 { exactCache.removeAll(keepingCapacity: true) }
            exactCache[raw] = computed
        }
        return computed
    }
}

// MARK: - Lightweight Multi-Language Syntax Highlighter (Single Selectable AttributedString!)
public enum CodeSyntaxHighlighter {
    private static let keywords: Set<String> = [
        "func", "let", "var", "struct", "class", "enum", "actor", "protocol", "extension",
        "import", "return", "if", "else", "switch", "case", "default", "for", "while",
        "guard", "try", "catch", "throw", "throws", "async", "await", "public", "private",
        "internal", "static", "self", "nil", "true", "false", "init", "deinit", "some", "any",
        "def", "from", "as", "with", "yield", "lambda", "None", "True", "False", "pass", "elif",
        "const", "function", "export", "interface", "type", "new", "null", "undefined",
        "fn", "mut", "pub", "impl", "match", "use", "mod", "crate", "SELECT", "FROM", "WHERE",
        "INSERT", "INTO", "VALUES", "CREATE", "TABLE", "DELETE", "UPDATE", "SET", "ORDER", "BY"
    ]

    public static func highlight(code: String, isDark: Bool, baseColor: Color) -> AttributedString {
        var result = AttributedString(code)
        result.foregroundColor = baseColor
        result.font = .system(size: 12.5, weight: .regular, design: .monospaced)

        let kwColor = isDark ? Color(red: 0.78, green: 0.56, blue: 0.98) : Color(red: 0.58, green: 0.14, blue: 0.58)
        let strColor = isDark ? Color(red: 0.96, green: 0.54, blue: 0.48) : Color(red: 0.76, green: 0.16, blue: 0.12)
        let commentColor = isDark ? Color(red: 0.48, green: 0.54, blue: 0.62) : Color(red: 0.42, green: 0.47, blue: 0.52)
        let numColor = isDark ? Color(red: 0.48, green: 0.78, blue: 0.96) : Color(red: 0.11, green: 0.36, blue: 0.74)

        let nsCode = code as NSString
        let fullNSRange = NSRange(location: 0, length: nsCode.length)

        func applyPattern(_ pattern: String, color: Color, options: NSRegularExpression.Options = [], filter: ((String) -> Bool)? = nil) {
            guard let regex = try? NSRegularExpression(pattern: pattern, options: options) else { return }
            for match in regex.matches(in: code, range: fullNSRange) {
                guard let swiftRange = Range(match.range, in: code) else { continue }
                if let filter = filter, !filter(String(code[swiftRange])) { continue }
                if let attrRange = Range(swiftRange, in: result) {
                    result[attrRange].foregroundColor = color
                }
            }
        }

        // Order matters: keywords & numbers first, then strings, then comments on top
        applyPattern("\\b[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*\\b", color: kwColor) { keywords.contains($0) }
        applyPattern("\\b\\d+(\\.\\d+)?\\b", color: numColor)
        applyPattern("\"([^\"\\\\]|\\\\.)*\"|'([^'\\\\]|\\\\.)*'", color: strColor)
        applyPattern("(//[^\\n]*|#[^\\n]*)", color: commentColor)

        return result
    }
}

public struct StreamSafeMarkdownView: View {
    public let rawMarkdown: String
    public let isStreaming: Bool
    public let effectiveFontSize: CGFloat
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    public let typo: TypographyConfiguration
    public var useMonospaceBody: Bool = false
    public var overrideTextColor: Color? = nil
    public let onOpenCodeInInspector: (String, String) -> Void

    public var body: some View {
        let blocks = MarkdownParseCache.shared.blocks(for: rawMarkdown, isStreaming: isStreaming) {
            parseBlocks(from: sanitizeIncompleteStreamMarkers(rawMarkdown, isStreaming: isStreaming))
        }
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: useMonospaceBody ? 8 : 12) {
            ForEach(blocks) { block in
                switch block.kind {
                case .codeBlock(let lang, let code):
                    MotifCodeBlockView(
                        language: lang,
                        code: code,
                        tokens: tokens,
                        typo: typo,
                        onOpenInArtifactPane: { onOpenCodeInInspector(lang, code) }
                    )
                    .padding(.vertical, 2)

                case .table(let headers, let rows):
                    markdownTableView(headers: headers, rows: rows)

                case .heading(let level, let text):
                    let size: CGFloat = {
                        switch level {
                        case 1: return effectiveFontSize + 3.6
                        case 2: return effectiveFontSize + 2.0
                        case 3: return effectiveFontSize + 0.8
                        default: return effectiveFontSize + 0.2
                        }
                    }()
                    renderInlineRichText(text, baseFontSize: size, isHeading: true)
                        .padding(.top, level <= 3 ? 3 : 1)

                case .bulletItem(let indent, let text):
                    HStack(alignment: .firstTextBaseline, spacing: 9) {
                        Text(indent > 0 ? "◦" : "•")
                            .font(typo.mono(size: max(11, effectiveFontSize - 1), weight: .medium))
                            .foregroundColor(overrideTextColor ?? tokens.secondaryText)
                            .frame(width: 12, alignment: .trailing)
                        renderInlineRichText(text, baseFontSize: effectiveFontSize, isHeading: false)
                    }
                    .padding(.leading, CGFloat(min(indent, 4)) * 16.0)

                case .numberedItem(let indent, let numberStr, let text):
                    HStack(alignment: .firstTextBaseline, spacing: 8) {
                        Text(numberStr)
                            .font(typo.sans(size: max(11, effectiveFontSize - 0.8), weight: .semibold).monospacedDigit())
                            .foregroundColor(overrideTextColor ?? tokens.secondaryText)
                            .frame(width: 24, alignment: .trailing)
                        renderInlineRichText(text, baseFontSize: effectiveFontSize, isHeading: false)
                    }
                    .padding(.leading, CGFloat(min(indent, 4)) * 16.0)

                case .blockquote(let text):
                    HStack(spacing: 12) {
                        Rectangle().fill(tokens.strongBorder).frame(width: 2)
                        renderInlineRichText(text, baseFontSize: effectiveFontSize, isHeading: false)
                            .foregroundColor(overrideTextColor ?? tokens.secondaryText)
                    }
                    .padding(.vertical, 2)

                case .horizontalRule:
                    HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens).padding(.vertical, 4)

                case .paragraph(let text):
                    renderInlineRichText(text, baseFontSize: effectiveFontSize, isHeading: false)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func markdownTableView(headers: [String], rows: [[String]]) -> some View {
        ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
            Grid(alignment: .leading, horizontalSpacing: 0, verticalSpacing: 0) {
                GridRow {
                    ForEach(Array(headers.enumerated()), id: \.offset) { _, header in
                        renderInlineRichText(header, baseFontSize: max(11.5, effectiveFontSize - 1.0), isHeading: true)
                            .padding(.horizontal, 12)
                            .padding(.vertical, 7)
                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                    }
                }
                .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)

                ForEach(Array(rows.enumerated()), id: \.offset) { rowIdx, row in
                    HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)
                    GridRow {
                        ForEach(0..<headers.count, id: \.self) { colIdx in
                            let cell = colIdx < row.count ? row[colIdx] : ""
                            renderInlineRichText(cell, baseFontSize: max(11.5, effectiveFontSize - 1.0), isHeading: false)
                                .padding(.horizontal, 12)
                                .padding(.vertical, 6)
                                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                        }
                    }
                    .background(rowIdx % 2 == 1 ? tokens.elevatedSurface.opacity(0.32) : Color.clear)
                }
            }
            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous))
            .overlay(
                RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous)
                    .strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1)
            )
        }
        .padding(.vertical, 3)
    }

    private func sanitizeIncompleteStreamMarkers(_ input: String, isStreaming: Bool) -> String {
        guard isStreaming else { return input }
        var result = input
        let boldCount = result.components(separatedBy: "**").count - 1
        if boldCount % 2 != 0, let lastRange = result.range(of: "**", options: .backwards) {
            result.removeSubrange(lastRange)
        }
        return result
    }

    private func matchHeading(_ trimmed: String) -> (level: Int, text: String)? {
        guard trimmed.hasPrefix("#") else { return nil }
        var count = 0
        for ch in trimmed { if ch == "#" { count += 1 } else { break } }
        guard count >= 1 && count <= 6 else { return nil }
        let remainder = String(trimmed.dropFirst(count))
        guard remainder.isEmpty || remainder.first == " " || remainder.first == "\t" else { return nil }
        var cleaned = remainder.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        while cleaned.hasSuffix("#") { cleaned.removeLast() }
        cleaned = cleaned.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        return cleaned.isEmpty ? nil : (count, cleaned)
    }

    private func splitTableRow(_ line: String) -> [String] {
        var trimmed = line.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        if trimmed.hasPrefix("|") { trimmed.removeFirst() }
        if trimmed.hasSuffix("|") { trimmed.removeLast() }
        return trimmed.components(separatedBy: "|").map { $0.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces) }
    }

    private func isTableSeparatorLine(_ line: String) -> Bool {
        let trimmed = line.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        guard trimmed.contains("-") && trimmed.contains("|") else { return false }
        let allowed = CharacterSet(charactersIn: "|-: ")
        return trimmed.unicodeScalars.allSatisfy { allowed.contains($0) }
    }

    /// Line-by-line fence-aware parser: handles nested fences (``` / ```` / ~~~), tables, and indented lists.
    private func parseBlocks(from raw: String) -> [MarkdownBlock] {
        let lines = raw.components(separatedBy: .newlines)
        var blocks: [MarkdownBlock] = []
        var paragraphBuffer: [String] = []
        var idx = 0

        func flushParagraph() {
            let joined = paragraphBuffer.joined(separator: "\n").trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
            if !joined.isEmpty {
                blocks.append(MarkdownBlock(id: "p_\(blocks.count)", kind: .paragraph(joined)))
            }
            paragraphBuffer.removeAll()
        }

        while idx < lines.count {
            let line = lines[idx]
            let trimmed = line.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)

            // Fenced code block (supports 3+ backticks or tildes)
            if trimmed.hasPrefix("```") || trimmed.hasPrefix("~~~") {
                flushParagraph()
                let fenceChar = trimmed.first!
                let fenceLen = trimmed.prefix(while: { $0 == fenceChar }).count
                let lang = String(trimmed.dropFirst(fenceLen)).trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
                var codeLines: [String] = []
                idx += 1
                while idx < lines.count {
                    let candidate = lines[idx].trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
                    if candidate.prefix(while: { $0 == fenceChar }).count >= fenceLen
                        && candidate.drop(while: { $0 == fenceChar }).trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty {
                        idx += 1
                        break
                    }
                    codeLines.append(lines[idx])
                    idx += 1
                }
                blocks.append(MarkdownBlock(id: "code_\(blocks.count)", kind: .codeBlock(lang, codeLines.joined(separator: "\n"))))
                continue
            }

            // Markdown Table detection (header + separator row)
            if trimmed.contains("|"), idx + 1 < lines.count, isTableSeparatorLine(lines[idx + 1]) {
                flushParagraph()
                let headers = splitTableRow(trimmed)
                var rows: [[String]] = []
                idx += 2
                while idx < lines.count {
                    let rowLine = lines[idx].trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
                    if rowLine.isEmpty || !rowLine.contains("|") { break }
                    rows.append(splitTableRow(rowLine))
                    idx += 1
                }
                blocks.append(MarkdownBlock(id: "tbl_\(blocks.count)", kind: .table(headers, rows)))
                continue
            }

            let leadingSpaces = line.prefix(while: { $0 == " " || $0 == "\t" }).reduce(0) { $0 + ($1 == "\t" ? 4 : 1) }
            let indentLevel = leadingSpaces / 2

            if trimmed.isEmpty {
                flushParagraph()
            } else if trimmed == "---" || trimmed == "***" || trimmed == "___" {
                flushParagraph()
                blocks.append(MarkdownBlock(id: "hr_\(blocks.count)", kind: .horizontalRule))
            } else if let heading = matchHeading(trimmed) {
                flushParagraph()
                blocks.append(MarkdownBlock(id: "h_\(blocks.count)", kind: .heading(heading.level, heading.text)))
            } else if trimmed.hasPrefix("- ") || trimmed.hasPrefix("* ") || trimmed.hasPrefix("• ") || trimmed.hasPrefix("+ ") {
                flushParagraph()
                blocks.append(MarkdownBlock(id: "b_\(blocks.count)", kind: .bulletItem(indentLevel, String(trimmed.dropFirst(2)))))
            } else if trimmed.hasPrefix("> ") || trimmed == ">" {
                flushParagraph()
                let quoteBody = trimmed.count > 2 ? String(trimmed.dropFirst(2)) : ""
                if !quoteBody.isEmpty {
                    blocks.append(MarkdownBlock(id: "q_\(blocks.count)", kind: .blockquote(quoteBody)))
                }
            } else if let num = matchNumberedList(trimmed) {
                flushParagraph()
                blocks.append(MarkdownBlock(id: "n_\(blocks.count)", kind: .numberedItem(indentLevel, num.number, num.rest)))
            } else {
                paragraphBuffer.append(line)
            }
            idx += 1
        }
        flushParagraph()
        return blocks
    }

    private func matchNumberedList(_ line: String) -> (number: String, rest: String)? {
        guard let sepIdx = line.firstIndex(of: ".") ?? line.firstIndex(of: ")") else { return nil }
        let prefix = String(line[..<sepIdx])
        guard !prefix.isEmpty, prefix.allSatisfy({ $0.isNumber }) else { return nil }
        let afterSep = line.index(after: sepIdx)
        guard afterSep < line.endIndex, line[afterSep] == " " else { return nil }
        return ("\(prefix).", String(line[line.index(after: afterSep)...]))
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func renderInlineRichText(_ text: String, baseFontSize: CGFloat, isHeading: Bool) -> some View {
        Text(buildAttributedInline(text, baseFontSize: baseFontSize, isHeading: isHeading))
            .lineSpacing(CGFloat(baseFontSize * (useMonospaceBody ? 0.34 : (typo.lineSpacingMultiplier - 1.0))))
            .textSelection(.enabled)
            .fixedSize(horizontal: false, vertical: true)
    }

    private func buildAttributedInline(_ raw: String, baseFontSize: CGFloat, isHeading: Bool) -> AttributedString {
        var output = AttributedString()
        let parts = raw.components(separatedBy: "`")
        let targetColor = overrideTextColor ?? tokens.primaryText

        for (idx, part) in parts.enumerated() {
            if idx % 2 == 1 {
                var codeAttr = AttributedString(" \(part) ")
                codeAttr.font = .system(size: max(11.0, baseFontSize - 1.2), weight: .medium, design: .monospaced)
                codeAttr.foregroundColor = targetColor
                codeAttr.backgroundColor = tokens.elevatedSurface
                output.append(codeAttr)
            } else {
                let normalizedProse = LaTeXAndSymbolNormalizer.normalizeProse(part)
                var mdAttr = (try? AttributedString(markdown: normalizedProse, options: AttributedString.MarkdownParsingOptions(interpretedSyntax: .inlineOnlyPreservingWhitespace))) ?? AttributedString(normalizedProse)
                mdAttr.foregroundColor = targetColor
                for run in mdAttr.runs {
                    let range = run.range
                    let isBold = run.inlinePresentationIntent?.contains(.stronglyEmphasized) == true || isHeading
                    let isItalic = run.inlinePresentationIntent?.contains(.emphasized) == true
                    var f = useMonospaceBody
                        ? Font.system(size: baseFontSize, weight: isBold ? .bold : .regular, design: .monospaced)
                        : typo.sans(size: baseFontSize, weight: isBold ? .semibold : .regular)
                    if isItalic { f = f.italic() }
                    mdAttr[range].font = f
                }
                output.append(mdAttr)
            }
        }
        return output
    }
}

// MARK: - Syntax-Highlighted, Multi-Line-Selectable Code Block (Single AttributedString!)
public struct MotifCodeBlockView: View {
    public let language: String
    public let code: String
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    public let typo: TypographyConfiguration
    public let onOpenInArtifactPane: () -> Void

    @State private var isCopied: Bool = false
    @State private var softWrapEnabled: Bool = false

    private var trimmedCode: String { code.trimmingCharacters(in: .newlines) }
    private var lineCount: Int { max(1, trimmedCode.components(separatedBy: .newlines).count) }
    private var lineNumbersText: String { (1...lineCount).map(String.init).joined(separator: "\n") }

    public var body: some View {
        let highlighted = CodeSyntaxHighlighter.highlight(
            code: trimmedCode.isEmpty ? " " : trimmedCode,
            isDark: tokens.isEffectivelyDark,
            baseColor: tokens.primaryText
        )

        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 0) {
            HStack(spacing: 10) {
                Text(language.isEmpty ? "CODE" : language.uppercased())
                    .font(typo.mono(size: 10.5, weight: .semibold))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)

                Text("\(lineCount) \(lineCount == 1 ? "line" : "lines")")
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5).monospacedDigit())
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)

                Spacer()

                Button(softWrapEnabled ? "Unwrap" : "Wrap") { softWrapEnabled.toggle() }
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)

                Button("Inspector", action: onOpenInArtifactPane)
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)

                Button(action: copyCodeToClipboard) {
                    Text(isCopied ? "Copied" : "Copy")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(isCopied ? tokens.accentForeground : tokens.primaryText)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 8)
                        .padding(.vertical, 3)
                        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 4, style: .continuous).fill(isCopied ? tokens.accentAction : tokens.canvasBackground))
                        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 4, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 14)
            .padding(.vertical, 8)
            .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)

            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

            HStack(alignment: .top, spacing: 0) {
                if !softWrapEnabled {
                    Text(lineNumbersText)
                        .font(.system(size: 12.5, design: .monospaced).monospacedDigit())
                        .lineSpacing(3.5)
                        .multilineTextAlignment(.trailing)
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                        .frame(minWidth: 34, alignment: .trailing)
                        .padding(.vertical, 12)
                        .padding(.trailing, 10)
                        .padding(.leading, 8)
                        .background(tokens.sidebarBackground)

                    HairlineDivider(axis: .vertical, tokens: tokens)
                }

                if softWrapEnabled {
                    Text(highlighted)
                        .lineSpacing(3.5)
                        .textSelection(.enabled)
                        .fixedSize(horizontal: false, vertical: true)
                        .padding(.vertical, 12)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 16)
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                } else {
                    ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                        Text(highlighted)
                            .lineSpacing(3.5)
                            .textSelection(.enabled)
                            .fixedSize(horizontal: true, vertical: true)
                            .padding(.vertical, 12)
                            .padding(.leading, 16)
                            .padding(.trailing, 24)
                    }
                }
            }
            .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
        }
        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous))
        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
    }

    private func copyCodeToClipboard() {
        NSPasteboard.general.clearContents()
        NSPasteboard.general.setString(trimmedCode, forType: .string)
        withAnimation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.14)) { isCopied = true }
        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 1.5) {
            withAnimation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.14)) { isCopied = false }
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Optional Sampling Slider & Stepper Controls (Preserves Modelfile Defaults When Nil!)
public struct OptionalParameterSliderRow: View {
    public let title: String
    public let explanation: String
    @Binding public var value: Double?
    public let fallbackValue: Double
    public let range: ClosedRange<Double>
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    public let typo: TypographyConfiguration
    public var onCommit: () -> Void = {}

    public var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
            HStack(spacing: 6) {
                Text(title)
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    .help(explanation)

                Spacer()

                if let v = value {
                    Text(String(format: "%.2f", v))
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .semibold).monospacedDigit())
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.accentAction)
                    Button {
                        value = nil
                        onCommit()
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "arrow.counterclockwise")
                            .font(.system(size: 9.5, weight: .semibold))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    .help("Reset to model's native Modelfile default")
                } else {
                    Text("Model Default")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                }
            }

            Slider(
                value: Binding(
                    get: { value ?? fallbackValue },
                    set: {
                        value = $0
                        onCommit()
                    }
                ),
                in: range
            )
            .tint(value == nil ? tokens.mutedText.opacity(0.45) : tokens.accentAction)
        }
    }
}

public struct ParameterIntStepperRow: View {
    public let title: String
    public let explanation: String
    @Binding public var value: Int
    public let defaultValue: Int
    public let range: ClosedRange<Int>
    public let step: Int
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    public let typo: TypographyConfiguration
    public var onCommit: () -> Void = {}

    public var body: some View {
        HStack(spacing: 8) {
            Text(title)
                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                .help(explanation)
            Spacer()
            if value != defaultValue {
                Button {
                    value = defaultValue
                    onCommit()
                } label: {
                    Image(systemName: "arrow.counterclockwise")
                        .font(.system(size: 9.5, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                .help("Reset to \(defaultValue)")
            }
            Text("\(value)")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold).monospacedDigit())
                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                .frame(minWidth: 44, alignment: .trailing)
            Stepper("", value: Binding(get: { value }, set: { value = $0; onCommit() }), in: range, step: step)
                .labelsHidden()
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Theme-Matched Multi-Line Text Editor (`MotifStyledTextEditor`)
public struct MotifStyledTextEditor: NSViewRepresentable {
    @Binding public var text: String
    public let font: NSFont
    public let tokens: MotifTokens

    public init(text: Binding<String>, font: NSFont, tokens: MotifTokens) {
        self._text = text
        self.font = font
        self.tokens = tokens
    }

    public func makeCoordinator() -> Coordinator { Coordinator(self) }

    public func makeNSView(context: Context) -> NSScrollView {
        let scrollView = NSScrollView()
        scrollView.drawsBackground = false
        scrollView.hasVerticalScroller = true
        scrollView.hasHorizontalScroller = false
        scrollView.autohidesScrollers = true
        scrollView.scrollerStyle = .overlay
        scrollView.borderType = .noBorder
        scrollView.appearance = NSAppearance(named: tokens.isEffectivelyDark ? .darkAqua : .aqua)

        let textView = NSTextView()
        textView.delegate = context.coordinator
        textView.drawsBackground = false
        textView.isRichText = false
        textView.allowsUndo = true
        textView.isAutomaticQuoteSubstitutionEnabled = false
        textView.isAutomaticDashSubstitutionEnabled = false
        textView.font = font
        textView.textColor = NSColor(tokens.primaryText)
        textView.insertionPointColor = NSColor(tokens.primaryText)
        textView.textContainerInset = NSSize(width: 4, height: 6)
        textView.isVerticallyResizable = true
        textView.isHorizontallyResizable = false
        textView.autoresizingMask = [.width]
        textView.textContainer?.widthTracksTextView = true

        scrollView.documentView = textView
        return scrollView
    }

    public func updateNSView(_ scrollView: NSScrollView, context: Context) {
        context.coordinator.parent = self
        scrollView.scrollerStyle = .overlay
        scrollView.appearance = NSAppearance(named: tokens.isEffectivelyDark ? .darkAqua : .aqua)
        guard let textView = scrollView.documentView as? NSTextView else { return }
        if textView.string != text && !textView.hasMarkedText() { textView.string = text }
        if textView.font != font { textView.font = font }
        let targetColor = NSColor(tokens.primaryText)
        if textView.textColor != targetColor {
            textView.textColor = targetColor
            textView.insertionPointColor = targetColor
        }
    }

    public final class Coordinator: NSObject, NSTextViewDelegate {
        var parent: MotifStyledTextEditor
        init(_ parent: MotifStyledTextEditor) { self.parent = parent }
        public func textDidChange(_ notification: Notification) {
            guard let tv = notification.object as? NSTextView, !tv.hasMarkedText() else { return }
            parent.text = tv.string
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - IME-Safe Quantized Composer NSTextView Bridge
public struct IMESafeComposerTextView: NSViewRepresentable {
    @Binding public var text: String
    @Binding public var isFocused: Bool
    @Binding public var compactHeight: CGFloat
    @Binding public var lineCount: Int
    public let placeholder: String
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    public let typo: TypographyConfiguration
    public let fontSize: CGFloat
    public let isSlashMenuVisible: Bool
    public let onSend: () -> Void
    public let onSlashMoveUp: () -> Void
    public let onSlashMoveDown: () -> Void
    public let onSlashConfirm: () -> Bool
    public let onSlashDismiss: () -> Void
    public let onLargePaste: (String) -> Void

    public func makeCoordinator() -> Coordinator { Coordinator(self) }

    public func makeNSView(context: Context) -> NSScrollView {
        let scrollView = NSScrollView()
        scrollView.drawsBackground = false
        scrollView.hasVerticalScroller = false
        scrollView.hasHorizontalScroller = false
        scrollView.borderType = .noBorder

        let textView = ComposerNSTextView()
        textView.delegate = context.coordinator
        textView.coordinator = context.coordinator
        textView.drawsBackground = false
        textView.isRichText = false
        textView.isAutomaticQuoteSubstitutionEnabled = false
        textView.isAutomaticDashSubstitutionEnabled = false
        textView.isAutomaticTextReplacementEnabled = false
        textView.isAutomaticSpellingCorrectionEnabled = false
        textView.allowsUndo = true
        textView.font = typo.nsFont(size: fontSize)
        textView.textContainerInset = NSSize(width: 2, height: 4)
        textView.isVerticallyResizable = true
        textView.isHorizontallyResizable = false
        textView.autoresizingMask = [.width]
        textView.textContainer?.widthTracksTextView = true
        textView.textContainer?.lineFragmentPadding = 4

        scrollView.documentView = textView
        return scrollView
    }

    public func updateNSView(_ scrollView: NSScrollView, context: Context) {
        context.coordinator.parent = self
        guard let textView = scrollView.documentView as? ComposerNSTextView else { return }
        let textChangedExternally = (textView.string != text && !textView.hasMarkedText())
        if textChangedExternally {
            textView.string = text
            textView.needsDisplay = true
        }
        let targetFont = typo.nsFont(size: fontSize)
        if textView.font != targetFont { textView.font = targetFont }
        let targetTextColor = NSColor(tokens.primaryText)
        if textView.textColor != targetTextColor {
            textView.textColor = targetTextColor
            textView.insertionPointColor = targetTextColor
        }
        let targetPlaceholderColor = NSColor(tokens.mutedText)
        if textView.placeholderString != placeholder || textView.placeholderColor != targetPlaceholderColor {
            textView.placeholderString = placeholder
            textView.placeholderColor = targetPlaceholderColor
            if textView.string.isEmpty { textView.needsDisplay = true }
        }
        if textChangedExternally {
            context.coordinator.recalculateQuantizedMetrics(for: textView)
        }
    }

    public final class Coordinator: NSObject, NSTextViewDelegate {
        var parent: IMESafeComposerTextView
        private var lastMeasuredWidth: CGFloat = 0

        init(_ parent: IMESafeComposerTextView) { self.parent = parent }
        public func textDidBeginEditing(_ notification: Notification) { parent.isFocused = true }
        public func textDidEndEditing(_ notification: Notification) { parent.isFocused = false }

        public func textDidChange(_ notification: Notification) {
            guard let tv = notification.object as? NSTextView else { return }
            if !tv.hasMarkedText() { parent.text = tv.string }
            recalculateQuantizedMetrics(for: tv)
        }

        public func handleWidthChangeIfNeeded(for textView: NSTextView) {
            let currentW = textView.bounds.width
            guard abs(currentW - lastMeasuredWidth) > 6.0 else { return }
            lastMeasuredWidth = currentW
            recalculateQuantizedMetrics(for: textView)
        }

        public func recalculateQuantizedMetrics(for textView: NSTextView) {
            if textView.string.isEmpty {
                if parent.lineCount != 1 || parent.compactHeight != 28.0 {
                    DispatchQueue.main.async {
                        self.parent.lineCount = 1
                        self.parent.compactHeight = 28.0
                    }
                }
                return
            }
            guard let lm = textView.layoutManager, let tc = textView.textContainer else { return }
            lm.ensureLayout(for: tc)
            let usedH = lm.usedRect(for: tc).height
            let singleH = max(18.0, (textView.font?.ascender ?? 13.0) - (textView.font?.descender ?? -4.0) + (textView.font?.leading ?? 2.0))
            let lines = max(1, Int(round(usedH / singleH)))
            let targetH: CGFloat = lines <= 1 ? 28.0 : (lines == 2 ? 46.0 : 64.0)
            if parent.lineCount != lines || abs(parent.compactHeight - targetH) > 0.5 {
                DispatchQueue.main.async {
                    self.parent.lineCount = lines
                    self.parent.compactHeight = targetH
                }
            }
        }

        public func textView(_ textView: NSTextView, doCommandBy commandSelector: Selector) -> Bool {
            if textView.hasMarkedText() { return false }
            if parent.isSlashMenuVisible {
                if commandSelector == #selector(NSResponder.moveUp(_:)) { parent.onSlashMoveUp(); return true }
                if commandSelector == #selector(NSResponder.moveDown(_:)) { parent.onSlashMoveDown(); return true }
                if commandSelector == #selector(NSResponder.insertTab(_:)) {
                    let ok = parent.onSlashConfirm()
                    recalculateQuantizedMetrics(for: textView)
                    return ok
                }
                if commandSelector == #selector(NSResponder.cancelOperation(_:)) { parent.onSlashDismiss(); return true }
            }
            if commandSelector == #selector(NSResponder.insertNewline(_:)) {
                let flags = NSApp.currentEvent?.modifierFlags ?? []
                if flags.contains(.shift) {
                    textView.insertNewlineIgnoringFieldEditor(nil)
                    recalculateQuantizedMetrics(for: textView)
                    return true
                } else {
                    if parent.isSlashMenuVisible && parent.onSlashConfirm() {
                        recalculateQuantizedMetrics(for: textView)
                        return true
                    }
                    parent.onSend()
                    return true
                }
            }
            return false
        }
    }
}

public final class ComposerNSTextView: NSTextView {
    weak var coordinator: IMESafeComposerTextView.Coordinator?
    var placeholderString: String = ""
    var placeholderColor: NSColor = .secondaryLabelColor

    public override var acceptsFirstResponder: Bool { true }

    public override func mouseDown(with event: NSEvent) {
        window?.makeFirstResponder(self)
        super.mouseDown(with: event)
        updateInsertionPointStateAndRestartTimer(true)
    }

    public override func becomeFirstResponder() -> Bool {
        let ok = super.becomeFirstResponder()
        if ok { updateInsertionPointStateAndRestartTimer(true) }
        return ok
    }

    public override func setFrameSize(_ newSize: NSSize) {
        super.setFrameSize(newSize)
        coordinator?.handleWidthChangeIfNeeded(for: self)
    }

    public override func draw(_ dirtyRect: NSRect) {
        super.draw(dirtyRect)
        if string.isEmpty && !hasMarkedText() {
            let attrs: [NSAttributedString.Key: Any] = [
                .font: font ?? NSFont.systemFont(ofSize: 14),
                .foregroundColor: placeholderColor
            ]
            (placeholderString as NSString).draw(in: NSRect(x: 6, y: 4, width: bounds.width - 12, height: bounds.height - 6), withAttributes: attrs)
        }
    }

    public override func paste(_ sender: Any?) {
        if let pasted = NSPasteboard.general.string(forType: .string),
           (pasted.count > 2400 || pasted.components(separatedBy: .newlines).count > 55) {
            // Appends as chip WITHOUT touching the existing draft text!
            coordinator?.parent.onLargePaste(pasted)
            return
        }
        super.pasteAsPlainText(sender)
        coordinator?.recalculateQuantizedMetrics(for: self)
    }
}
EOF
)

                if !focused, renamingSessionID != nil { commitActiveRenameIfNeeded() }

        .overlay(alignment: .trailing) {

        }
    }

    private func commitActiveRenameIfNeeded() {
        if let activeFolderID = renamingFolderID {
            store.renameWorkspace(folderID: activeFolderID, newName: folderRenameDraft)
            renamingFolderID = nil
        }
        guard let activeID = renamingSessionID else { return }
        store.commitInlineRename(sessionID: activeID, proposedName: renameDraft)
        renamingSessionID = nil
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func chatRowItem(_ session: ConversationSession, indented: Bool) -> some View {
        let isSelected = store.selectedSessionID == session.id
        let isGeneratingHere = store.isStreamingGeneration && store.streamingSessionID == session.id
        let isBranchedChat = session.title.lowercased().hasPrefix("branch")

        if renamingSessionID == session.id {
            HStack(spacing: 6) {
                TextField("Chat title", text: $renameDraft)
                    .textFieldStyle(.plain)
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 13, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    .focused($isRenameFocused)
                    .onSubmit { commitActiveRenameIfNeeded() }
                    .onExitCommand { renamingSessionID = nil }
            }
            .padding(.leading, indented ? 22 : 10)
            .padding(.trailing, 10)
            .frame(height: 30)
            .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.elevatedSurface))
            .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.accentAction, lineWidth: 1))
            .padding(.horizontal, 8)
        } else {
            Button {
                commitActiveRenameIfNeeded()
                store.selectedSessionID = session.id
            } label: {
                HStack(spacing: 6) {
                    if isBranchedChat {
                        Image(systemName: "arrow.triangle.branch")
                            .font(.system(size: 10, weight: .semibold))
                            .foregroundColor(isSelected ? tokens.accentAction : tokens.mutedText)
                    }
                    Text(session.title)
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 13, weight: isSelected ? .medium : .regular))
                        .foregroundColor(isSelected ? tokens.primaryText : tokens.secondaryText)
                        .lineLimit(1)
                    Spacer(minLength: 4)
                    if isGeneratingHere {
                        Circle().fill(tokens.statusOnline).frame(width: 6, height: 6)
                    }
                }
                .padding(.leading, indented ? 22 : 10)
                .padding(.trailing, 10)
                .frame(height: 30)
                .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(isSelected ? tokens.selectionPill : Color.clear))
                .contentShape(Rectangle())
            }
            .buttonStyle(.plain)
            .padding(.horizontal, 8)
            .contextMenu {
                Button("Rename") {
                    commitActiveRenameIfNeeded()
                    renameDraft = session.title
                    renamingSessionID = session.id
                    DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.05) { isRenameFocused = true }
                }
                Button("Duplicate") { store.duplicateSession(session.id) }
                Button("Export as Markdown…") { store.exportSessionAsMarkdown(session.id) }
                if !store.folders.isEmpty {
                    Menu("Move to Workspace") {
                        Button("None (Conversations)") {
                            if let idx = store.sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == session.id }) {
                                store.sessions[idx].folderID = nil
                                store.saveAllState()
                            }
                        }
                        ForEach(store.folders) { folder in
                            Button(folder.name) {
                                if let idx = store.sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == session.id }) {
                                    store.sessions[idx].folderID = folder.id
                                    store.saveAllState()
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                Divider()
                Button("Move to Trash", role: .destructive) { store.moveSessionToTrash(session.id) }
            }
        }
    }
}
