import SwiftUI
import AppKit

private struct ComposerHeightPreferenceKey: PreferenceKey {
    static var defaultValue: CGFloat = 86.0
    static func reduce(value: inout CGFloat, nextValue: () -> CGFloat) {
        value = max(value, nextValue())
    }
}

public struct EditorialConversationView: View {
    @ObservedObject public var store: WorkstationStore
    public let tokens: MotifTokens

    @State private var editingMessageNodeID: UUID? = nil
    @State private var editMessageDraft: String = ""
    @State private var clearThinkingOnAssistantEdit: Bool = false
    @State private var isEditBoxExpandedFull: Bool = false
    @State private var isNewChatAdvancedExpanded: Bool = false
    @State private var copiedMessageID: UUID? = nil
    @State private var confirmingDeleteNodeID: UUID? = nil
    @State private var composerMeasuredHeight: CGFloat = 86.0

    private var typo: TypographyConfiguration { store.typography }

    private let newChatPresetGridColumns = [
        GridItem(.adaptive(minimum: 112, maximum: 180), spacing: 6)
    ]

    public var body: some View {
        GeometryReader { geo in
            let canvasWidth = geo.size.width
            let isFullscreenOrWide = store.isWindowFullscreen || canvasWidth >= 1320
            let isCompactWindow = canvasWidth < 960
            let useLeftMetadataGutter = canvasWidth >= 1120
            let horizontalOuterPadding: CGFloat = canvasWidth < 1040 ? 18.0 : 28.0

            let opticalFontSize = typo.effectiveFontSize(forCanvasWidth: canvasWidth, isFullscreen: isFullscreenOrWide)
            let adaptiveProseWidth = typo.effectiveReadingWidth(forCanvasWidth: canvasWidth, isFullscreen: isFullscreenOrWide)
            let totalRowMaxWidth: CGFloat = useLeftMetadataGutter
                ? min(canvasWidth - (horizontalOuterPadding * 2), adaptiveProseWidth + 148)
                : adaptiveProseWidth

            VStack(spacing: 0) {
                topHeaderBar(isCompactWindow: isCompactWindow)
                HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                if let errBanner = store.humanReadableErrorBanner {
                    errorNoticeBanner(text: errBanner)
                }

                if let session = store.activeSession {
                    contextAndMemoryTopBanner(session: session)

                    ZStack(alignment: .bottom) {
                        if session.activeBranchThread.isEmpty {
                            newChatPreFlightCanvas(session: session, maxCardWidth: min(totalRowMaxWidth, 640))
                        } else {
                            conversationStreamCanvas(
                                session: session,
                                useLeftGutter: useLeftMetadataGutter,
                                totalRowMaxWidth: totalRowMaxWidth,
                                horizontalPadding: horizontalOuterPadding,
                                opticalFontSize: opticalFontSize,
                                isFullscreenOrWide: isFullscreenOrWide
                            )
                        }

                        ProgressiveComposerBar(
                            store: store,
                            tokens: tokens,
                            maxBarWidth: totalRowMaxWidth,
                            activeFontSize: opticalFontSize,
                            isWideOrFullscreen: isFullscreenOrWide
                        )
                        .padding(.horizontal, horizontalOuterPadding)
                        .padding(.bottom, 14)
                        .background(
                            GeometryReader { compGeo in
                                Color.clear.preference(key: ComposerHeightPreferenceKey.self, value: compGeo.size.height)
                            }
                        )
                    }
                    .onPreferenceChange(ComposerHeightPreferenceKey.self) { newH in
                        if abs(composerMeasuredHeight - newH) > 1.0 { composerMeasuredHeight = newH }
                    }
                } else {
                    strictNoSessionLandingGate
                }
            }
            .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Top Header Bar
    private func topHeaderBar(isCompactWindow: Bool) -> some View {
        let leadingClearance: CGFloat = (!store.isSidebarVisible && !store.isWindowFullscreen) ? 78.0 : 18.0
        let hasActiveMessages = !(store.activeSession?.activeBranchThread.isEmpty ?? true)

        return HStack(alignment: .center, spacing: 10) {
            if !store.isSidebarVisible {
                Button { store.isSidebarVisible = true } label: {
                    Image(systemName: "sidebar.left")
                        .font(.system(size: 12.5, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        .frame(width: 26, height: 26)
                        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.elevatedSurface))
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)

                Button { store.createNewSession(folderID: nil) } label: {
                    Image(systemName: "plus")
                        .font(.system(size: 12.5, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        .frame(width: 26, height: 26)
                        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.elevatedSurface))
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
            }

            Text(store.activeSession?.title ?? "Motif")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 13.5, weight: .semibold))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                .lineLimit(1)

            if let pillMsg = store.subtleStatusPillMessage {
                Text(pillMsg)
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.accentAction)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 8)
                    .padding(.vertical, 3)
                    .background(Capsule().fill(tokens.elevatedSurface))
            }

            Spacer(minLength: 8)

            if hasActiveMessages {
                livingModelSelectorPill(isCompactWindow: isCompactWindow)
            }

            HeaderMemoryIndicatorView(store: store, tokens: tokens, isCompactWindow: isCompactWindow)

            if store.activeSession != nil && !isCompactWindow {
                Button {
                    store.userManuallyClosedRightPaneInWideMode = store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen
                    store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen.toggle()
                } label: {
                    Image(systemName: "sidebar.right")
                        .font(.system(size: 12.5, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen ? tokens.accentAction : tokens.secondaryText)
                        .frame(width: 26, height: 26)
                        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.elevatedSurface.opacity(store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen ? 1.0 : 0.65)))
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                .help("Toggle Code Inspector (⌘\(store.shortcuts.toggleInspectorKey))")
            }
        }
        .padding(.leading, leadingClearance)
        .padding(.trailing, 18)
        .frame(height: 44)
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func livingModelSelectorPill(isCompactWindow: Bool) -> some View {
        let isLoading = store.isLoadingModelWeights
        let snap = store.memorySnapshot
        let loadFraction: CGFloat = isLoading ? CGFloat(min(0.99, max(0.02, snap.loadProgressAgainstTarget))) : 0.0
        let pct = Int(loadFraction * 100.0)
        let isResident = store.activeModelDescriptor?.residency == .activeInUnifiedRAM
        let flashOpacity = store.modelLoadCompletionRimFlashOpacity
        let isFlashingCompletion = flashOpacity > 0.01
        let activeModelTitle = isLoading
            ? LocalModelDescriptor.shortenModelName(store.loadingModelName)
            : (store.activeModelDescriptor?.shortDisplayName ?? "Select Model")

        HStack(spacing: 6) {
            if isLoading && store.isConfirmingModelLoadCancel {
                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                    Text("Cancel loading \(activeModelTitle)?")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    Button("Cancel Load") { store.confirmCancelModelLoad() }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 8).padding(.vertical, 3)
                        .background(Capsule().fill(tokens.accentAction))
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    Button("Keep") { store.isConfirmingModelLoadCancel = false }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, 12)
                .frame(height: 28)
                .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)
                .clipShape(Capsule())
                .overlay(Capsule().strokeBorder(tokens.accentAction.opacity(0.65), lineWidth: 1.5))
            } else {
                Button {
                    store.isModelPickerPopoverPresented.toggle()
                } label: {
                    HStack(spacing: 7) {
                        if isResident {
                            Circle().fill(tokens.statusOnline).frame(width: 6, height: 6)
                        } else if isLoading {
                            Circle().fill(tokens.accentAction).frame(width: 6, height: 6)
                        } else {
                            Circle().strokeBorder(tokens.mutedText, lineWidth: 1.3).frame(width: 6, height: 6)
                        }

                        Text(activeModelTitle)
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                            .lineLimit(1)
                            .frame(maxWidth: isCompactWindow ? 130 : 195, alignment: .leading)

                        if isLoading {
                            Text("\(pct)%")
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .semibold).monospacedDigit())
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.accentAction)
                        } else {
                            Image(systemName: "chevron.down")
                                .font(.system(size: 8.5, weight: .semibold))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal, 12)
                    .frame(height: 28)
                    .background(
                        GeometryReader { pillGeo in
                            ZStack(alignment: .leading) {
                                Rectangle().fill(isFlashingCompletion ? tokens.statusOnline.opacity(0.14) : tokens.canvasBackground)
                                if isLoading {
                                    Rectangle()
                                        .fill(tokens.accentAction.opacity(0.20))
                                        .frame(width: max(8, pillGeo.size.width * loadFraction))
                                        .animation(.easeOut(duration: 0.20), value: loadFraction)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    )
                    .clipShape(Capsule())
                    .overlay(
                        Capsule().strokeBorder(
                            isFlashingCompletion ? tokens.statusOnline.opacity(max(0.55, flashOpacity)) : (isLoading ? tokens.accentAction.opacity(0.75) : tokens.hairline),
                            lineWidth: isFlashingCompletion ? 1.8 : 1.0
                        )
                    )
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                .popover(isPresented: $store.isModelPickerPopoverPresented, arrowEdge: .bottom) {
                    ModelPickerPopoverView(store: store, tokens: tokens)
                }

                if isLoading {
                    Button { store.requestCancelModelLoadConfirmation() } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "xmark")
                            .font(.system(size: 9, weight: .bold))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                            .frame(width: 22, height: 22)
                            .background(Circle().fill(tokens.elevatedSurface))
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func contextAndMemoryTopBanner(session: ConversationSession) -> some View {
        let usedTok = session.estimatedActiveTokens
        let maxTok = max(1, session.parameters.numCtx)
        let ratio = Double(usedTok) / Double(maxTok)
        let hasMemWarn = store.kernelMemoryPressureWarning != nil
        let showContextWarn = ratio >= 0.85 && !store.isContextCeilingBannerDismissed

        if hasMemWarn || showContextWarn {
            VStack(spacing: 0) {
                HStack(spacing: 12) {
                    Circle().fill(tokens.statusWarning).frame(width: 6, height: 6)
                    if let memText = store.kernelMemoryPressureWarning {
                        Text(memText).font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    } else {
                        Text("Context window at \(Int(min(100, ratio * 100)))% (\(usedTok) / \(maxTok) tok)")
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                    }
                    Spacer()
                    Button("Trim Oldest Turn") { store.trimOldestTurnInActiveSession() }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 9).padding(.vertical, 3.5)
                        .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous))
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)

                    Button("Summarize into Branch") { store.summarizeContextIntoNewBranch() }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 9).padding(.vertical, 3.5)
                        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)

                    Button {
                        store.kernelMemoryPressureWarning = nil
                        store.isContextCeilingBannerDismissed = true
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "xmark").font(.system(size: 10, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, 20)
                .padding(.vertical, 7)
                .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)
                HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)
            }
        }
    }

    private func errorNoticeBanner(text: String) -> some View {
        HStack(spacing: 12) {
            Text(text).font(typo.sans(size: 12)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
            Spacer()
            if !store.supervisor.isEngineOnline {
                Button("Start Ollama") { Task { await store.bootstrapLocalOllamaConnection() } }
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
            }
            Button("Dismiss") { store.humanReadableErrorBanner = nil }
                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
        }
        .padding(.horizontal, 24)
        .padding(.vertical, 8)
        .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)
    }

    private var strictNoSessionLandingGate: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 16) {
            Spacer()
            Text("Motif").font(typo.wordmark(size: 30)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
            Text("Create a new chat to select a model and begin.")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 13.5))
                .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
            Button {
                store.createNewSession(folderID: nil)
            } label: {
                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                    Image(systemName: "plus").font(.system(size: 12, weight: .bold))
                    Text("New Chat").font(typo.sans(size: 13.5, weight: .semibold))
                }
                .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                .padding(.horizontal, 18).padding(.vertical, 9)
                .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
            }
            .buttonStyle(.plain)
            Spacer()
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, maxHeight: .infinity)
    }

    // MARK: - New-Chat Pre-Flight Studio Deck
    @ViewBuilder
    private func newChatPreFlightCanvas(session: ConversationSession, maxCardWidth: CGFloat) -> some View {
        let activeSessionIdx = store.sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == session.id })
        let model = store.activeModelDescriptor
        let isLoading = store.isLoadingModelWeights
        let snap = store.memorySnapshot
        let loadFraction: CGFloat = isLoading ? CGFloat(min(0.99, max(0.02, snap.loadProgressAgainstTarget))) : 0.0
        let pct = Int(loadFraction * 100.0)

        ScrollView(.vertical, showsIndicators: false) {
            VStack(spacing: 16) {
                Spacer(minLength: 14)
                Text(session.title).font(typo.sans(size: 20, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)

                if let sIdx = activeSessionIdx, let m = model {
                    let sessionBinding = $store.sessions[sIdx]
                    let profile = m.thinkingProfile
                    let isResident = m.residency == .activeInUnifiedRAM

                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                        HStack(alignment: .center, spacing: 10) {
                            Menu {
                                ForEach(store.availableModels) { candidate in
                                    Button("\(candidate.shortDisplayName) (\(candidate.residency == .activeInUnifiedRAM ? "In RAM" : candidate.formattedSize))") {
                                        store.selectModelOnly(candidate)
                                    }
                                }
                                Divider()
                                Button("Refresh Models") {
                                    Task {
                                        await store.refreshLocalEngineModels()
                                        await store.pollOllamaRunningProcesses()
                                    }
                                }
                                Button("Pull or Manage Models…") {
                                    store.settingsRequestedTab = "Models & Storage"
                                    store.isSettingsModalPresented = true
                                }
                            } label: {
                                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                                    Circle()
                                        .fill(isResident ? tokens.statusOnline : (isLoading ? tokens.accentAction : tokens.mutedText))
                                        .frame(width: 7, height: 7)
                                    Text(m.shortDisplayName)
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 13, weight: .semibold))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                        .lineLimit(1)
                                    Text("· \(m.formattedSize)")
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5).monospacedDigit())
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                    Image(systemName: "chevron.up.chevron.down")
                                        .font(.system(size: 9, weight: .semibold))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                                }
                                .padding(.horizontal, 12)
                                .frame(height: 30)
                                .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
                                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous))
                                .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.strongBorder.opacity(0.65), lineWidth: 1))
                            }
                            .menuStyle(.borderlessButton)

                            Spacer()

                            if isLoading {
                                Button("Cancel Load") { store.confirmCancelModelLoad() }
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                    .padding(.horizontal, 10).frame(height: 28)
                                    .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
                                    .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                            } else if isResident {
                                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                                    Text("In RAM").font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.statusOnline)
                                    Button("Unload") { Task { await store.unloadModelFromMemory(m) } }
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                        .padding(.horizontal, 9).frame(height: 26)
                                        .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
                                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                }
                            } else {
                                Button("Load into RAM") { store.selectAndLoadModel(m) }
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                                    .padding(.horizontal, 12).frame(height: 28)
                                    .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                            }
                        }

                        if isLoading {
                            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 5) {
                                HStack {
                                    Text("Loading \(store.loadingModelName) into Unified Memory…")
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                    Spacer()
                                    Text("\(pct)%")
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold).monospacedDigit())
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.accentAction)
                                }
                                ProgressView(value: Double(loadFraction)).tint(tokens.accentAction)
                            }
                        }

                        HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                        // Presets Grid
                        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                            HStack {
                                Text("Presets").font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                Spacer()
                                Button("Tight-RAM Optimize") { store.applyTightRAMOptimization() }
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .semibold))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                    .padding(.horizontal, 8).padding(.vertical, 3)
                                    .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
                                    .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous))
                                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                Button("+ Save Preset…") { store.isNewPresetSheetPresented = true }
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .semibold))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.accentAction)
                                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                            }

                            LazyVGrid(columns: newChatPresetGridColumns, alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
                                ForEach(store.presets) { preset in
                                    let isMatch = sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters == preset.parameters
                                    Button {
                                        sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters = preset.parameters
                                        sessionBinding.wrappedValue.systemPrompt = preset.systemPrompt
                                        store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference()
                                        if store.supervisor.kvCacheTypeEnv != preset.parameters.kvCacheQuant {
                                            store.applyKVCacheQuantizationLive(preset.parameters.kvCacheQuant)
                                        } else {
                                            store.recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
                                        }
                                    } label: {
                                        Text(preset.name)
                                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: isMatch ? .semibold : .medium))
                                            .foregroundColor(isMatch ? tokens.accentForeground : tokens.primaryText)
                                            .lineLimit(1)
                                            .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 5.5)
                                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                                            .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(isMatch ? tokens.accentAction : tokens.canvasBackground))
                                            .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(isMatch ? Color.clear : tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                                    }
                                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                    .help(preset.summary)
                                    .contextMenu {
                                        Button("Update “\(preset.name)” with Current Settings") { store.updatePresetWithCurrentSettings(preset.id) }
                                        Divider()
                                        Button("Delete Preset", role: .destructive) { store.deletePreset(preset.id) }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                        // Thinking Mode
                        HStack(alignment: .center, spacing: 12) {
                            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 1) {
                                Text("Thinking Mode").font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                Text(profile.detectedMechanismSummary).font(typo.sans(size: 10.5)).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                            }
                            Spacer()
                            if profile.kind != .unsupported || sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters.forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported {
                                let allowedModes: [ThinkingModeOption] = sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters.forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported
                                    ? [.off, .on, .level("low"), .level("medium"), .level("high")]
                                    : profile.validOptions

                                Picker("", selection: sessionBinding.parameters.thinkingMode) {
                                    ForEach(allowedModes) { mode in
                                        Text(mode.displayLabel).tag(mode)
                                    }
                                }
                                .pickerStyle(.segmented)
                                .labelsHidden()
                                .frame(width: allowedModes.count > 2 ? 220 : 130)
                                .onChange(of: sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters.thinkingMode) { _, _ in
                                    store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference()
                                }
                            } else {
                                Toggle("Force Override", isOn: sessionBinding.parameters.forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported)
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                            }
                        }

                        // Context Window (Capped to Model's Max Context!)
                        HStack(alignment: .center, spacing: 12) {
                            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 1) {
                                Text("Context Window").font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                Text("\(sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters.numCtx) tok · ~\(String(format: "%.2f GB", snap.kvCacheGB)) KV (max \(m.maxContextLength))")
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5).monospacedDigit())
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                            }
                            Spacer()
                            let tiers = [2048, 4096, 8192, 16384, 32768, 65536].filter { $0 <= max(4096, m.maxContextLength) }
                            Picker("", selection: sessionBinding.parameters.numCtx) {
                                ForEach(tiers, id: \.self) { t in
                                    Text("\(t / 1024)k").tag(t)
                                }
                            }
                            .pickerStyle(.segmented)
                            .labelsHidden()
                            .frame(width: 245)
                            .onChange(of: sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters.numCtx) { _, _ in
                                store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference()
                                store.recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
                            }
                        }

                        // KV Cache Precision
                        HStack(alignment: .center, spacing: 12) {
                            Text("KV Cache Precision").font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                            Spacer()
                            Picker("", selection: sessionBinding.parameters.kvCacheQuant) {
                                ForEach(KVCacheQuantization.allCases) { q in Text(q.displayTitle).tag(q) }
                            }
                            .pickerStyle(.menu)
                            .labelsHidden()
                            .frame(width: 240)
                            .onChange(of: sessionBinding.wrappedValue.parameters.kvCacheQuant) { _, newQuant in
                                store.applyKVCacheQuantizationLive(newQuant)
                            }
                        }

                        HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                        // Optional Sampling Overrides (Nil = Modelfile Default)
                        VStack(spacing: 10) {
                            HStack(spacing: 18) {
                                OptionalParameterSliderRow(
                                    title: "Temperature",
                                    explanation: "Leave at Model Default to preserve Modelfile tuning.",
                                    value: sessionBinding.parameters.temperature,
                                    fallbackValue: 0.70,
                                    range: 0.0...2.0,
                                    tokens: tokens,
                                    typo: typo,
                                    onCommit: { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
                                )
                                OptionalParameterSliderRow(
                                    title: "Top-P",
                                    explanation: "Nucleus sampling threshold.",
                                    value: sessionBinding.parameters.topP,
                                    fallbackValue: 0.90,
                                    range: 0.05...1.0,
                                    tokens: tokens,
                                    typo: typo,
                                    onCommit: { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
                                )
                            }
                            HStack(spacing: 18) {
                                OptionalParameterSliderRow(
                                    title: "Min-P",
                                    explanation: "Minimum probability cutoff relative to the top token.",
                                    value: sessionBinding.parameters.minP,
                                    fallbackValue: 0.05,
                                    range: 0.0...0.5,
                                    tokens: tokens,
                                    typo: typo,
                                    onCommit: { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
                                )
                                OptionalParameterSliderRow(
                                    title: "Repeat Penalty",
                                    explanation: "Penalizes repetitive loops.",
                                    value: sessionBinding.parameters.repeatPenalty,
                                    fallbackValue: 1.10,
                                    range: 0.8...1.6,
                                    tokens: tokens,
                                    typo: typo,
                                    onCommit: { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                        // System Prompt & Advanced Options
                        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
                            Button {
                                withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.14)) { isNewChatAdvancedExpanded.toggle() }
                            } label: {
                                HStack {
                                    Image(systemName: "chevron.right")
                                        .font(.system(size: 9.5, weight: .semibold))
                                        .rotationEffect(.degrees(isNewChatAdvancedExpanded ? 90 : 0))
                                    Text("System Prompt & Hardware Execution Options")
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
                                    Spacer()
                                    Text(isNewChatAdvancedExpanded ? "Hide" : "Configure")
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                }
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                .contentShape(Rectangle())
                            }
                            .buttonStyle(.plain)

                            if isNewChatAdvancedExpanded {
                                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
                                    Text("System Instructions (Keep static — avoid timestamps here to preserve KV prefix caching):")
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)

                                    MotifStyledTextEditor(text: sessionBinding.systemPrompt, font: typo.nsFont(size: 12), tokens: tokens)
                                        .frame(height: 64)
                                        .padding(4)
                                        .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
                                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                                        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))

                                    ParameterIntStepperRow(title: "Prompt Batch Size (num_batch)", explanation: "Lower values reduce Metal spike.", value: sessionBinding.parameters.numBatch, defaultValue: 512, range: 32...2048, step: 64, tokens: tokens, typo: typo) { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
                                    ParameterIntStepperRow(title: "Metal GPU Layers (-1 = Auto)", explanation: "-1 lets Ollama auto-fit GPU layers.", value: sessionBinding.parameters.numGpu, defaultValue: -1, range: -1...128, step: 1, tokens: tokens, typo: typo) { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
                                    ParameterIntStepperRow(title: "Keep-Alive Minutes", explanation: "Idle unload timer.", value: sessionBinding.parameters.keepAliveMinutes, defaultValue: 10, range: 0...120, step: 5, tokens: tokens, typo: typo) { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }

                                    HStack(spacing: 16) {
                                        Toggle("use_mmap", isOn: sessionBinding.parameters.useMmap).font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                                        Toggle("use_mlock", isOn: sessionBinding.parameters.useMlock).font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                                        Toggle("Structured JSON", isOn: sessionBinding.parameters.enforceJSONMode).font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(16)
                    .frame(maxWidth: maxCardWidth)
                    .background(tokens.elevatedSurface.opacity(0.75))
                    .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12, style: .continuous))
                    .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                } else {
                    Button("Open Settings to Pull or Configure Local Models…") {
                        store.settingsRequestedTab = "Models & Storage"
                        store.isSettingsModalPresented = true
                    }
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12.5, weight: .semibold))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.accentAction)
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }

                Spacer(minLength: 14)
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 24)
            .padding(.bottom, max(96, composerMeasuredHeight + 12))
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
            .background(OverlayScrollConfigurator(isDark: tokens.isEffectivelyDark).frame(width: 1, height: 1))
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, maxHeight: .infinity)
    }

    // MARK: - Conversation Stream Canvas
    private func conversationStreamCanvas(
        session: ConversationSession,
        useLeftGutter: Bool,
        totalRowMaxWidth: CGFloat,
        horizontalPadding: CGFloat,
        opticalFontSize: CGFloat,
        isFullscreenOrWide: Bool
    ) -> some View {
        let thread = session.activeBranchThread
        let lastTurnID = thread.last?.id
        let dynamicBottomClearance = max(130.0, composerMeasuredHeight + 28.0)
        let jumpButtonBottomOffset = max(96.0, composerMeasuredHeight + 12.0)

        return ScrollViewReader { proxy in
            ScrollView(.vertical, showsIndicators: true) {
                LazyVStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 26) {
                    ForEach(thread) { node in
                        messageTurnView(
                            node: node,
                            session: session,
                            isLatestTurn: node.id == lastTurnID,
                            useLeftGutter: useLeftGutter,
                            opticalFontSize: opticalFontSize,
                            isFullscreenOrWide: isFullscreenOrWide
                        )
                        .id(node.id)
                    }

                    Color.clear
                        .frame(height: dynamicBottomClearance)
                        .id("STREAM_BOTTOM_ANCHOR")
                }
                .background(
                    ScrollViewBottomObserver(
                        isScrolledAwayFromBottom: $store.isScrolledAwayFromBottom,
                        isDark: tokens.isEffectivelyDark
                    ).frame(width: 1, height: 1)
                )
                .frame(maxWidth: totalRowMaxWidth, alignment: .leading)
                .padding(.horizontal, horizontalPadding)
                .padding(.top, 22)
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
            }
            .onChange(of: store.streamScrollPulse) { _, _ in
                if store.isStreamingGeneration && !store.isScrolledAwayFromBottom && !store.isAutoScrollPaused {
                    proxy.scrollTo("STREAM_BOTTOM_ANCHOR", anchor: .bottom)
                }
            }
            .onChange(of: store.pendingScrollToMessageID) { _, targetID in
                if let id = targetID {
                    withAnimation(.spring(response: 0.28, dampingFraction: 0.86)) { proxy.scrollTo(id, anchor: .center) }
                    store.pendingScrollToMessageID = nil
                }
            }
            .overlay(alignment: .bottom) {
                if store.isScrolledAwayFromBottom && !store.isComposerCanvasExpanded && store.matchedSlashPersonas.isEmpty {
                    HighContrastJumpToLatestButton(tokens: tokens) {
                        store.isScrolledAwayFromBottom = false
                        store.isAutoScrollPaused = false
                        withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.18)) { proxy.scrollTo("STREAM_BOTTOM_ANCHOR", anchor: .bottom) }
                    }
                    .padding(.bottom, jumpButtonBottomOffset)
                    .transition(.scale.combined(with: .opacity))
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func messageTurnView(
        node: ConversationMessageNode,
        session: ConversationSession,
        isLatestTurn: Bool,
        useLeftGutter: Bool,
        opticalFontSize: CGFloat,
        isFullscreenOrWide: Bool
    ) -> some View {
        let siblingMeta = session.siblingInfo(for: node.id)
        let isNodeStreaming = store.isStreamingGeneration && store.streamingNodeID == node.id

        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            if useLeftGutter {
                HStack(alignment: .top, spacing: 20) {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 7) {
                        turnRoleOrHotSwapMenu(node: node, maxLabelWidth: 122)
                        if siblingMeta.totalCount > 1 { branchPaginationControl(nodeID: node.id, siblingMeta: siblingMeta) }
                        if isNodeStreaming {
                            Button("Stop") { store.stopActiveGeneration() }
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .semibold))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.accentAction)
                                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        }
                    }
                    .frame(width: 128, alignment: .leading)
                    .clipped()

                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
                        turnMainBody(node: node, isNodeStreaming: isNodeStreaming, opticalFontSize: opticalFontSize, isFullscreenOrWide: isFullscreenOrWide)
                        turnBottomActionBar(node: node, session: session, isLatestTurn: isLatestTurn, isNodeStreaming: isNodeStreaming)
                    }
                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                }
            } else {
                HStack(alignment: .center, spacing: 8) {
                    turnRoleOrHotSwapMenu(node: node, maxLabelWidth: 210)
                    if siblingMeta.totalCount > 1 { branchPaginationControl(nodeID: node.id, siblingMeta: siblingMeta) }
                    Spacer()
                    if isNodeStreaming {
                        Button("Stop") { store.stopActiveGeneration() }
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.accentAction)
                            .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    }
                }
                turnMainBody(node: node, isNodeStreaming: isNodeStreaming, opticalFontSize: opticalFontSize, isFullscreenOrWide: isFullscreenOrWide)
                turnBottomActionBar(node: node, session: session, isLatestTurn: isLatestTurn, isNodeStreaming: isNodeStreaming)
            }
            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens).padding(.top, 6)
        }
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func turnRoleOrHotSwapMenu(node: ConversationMessageNode, maxLabelWidth: CGFloat) -> some View {
        if node.role == .user {
            Text("You").font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
        } else {
            let shortLabel = LocalModelDescriptor.shortenModelName(node.modelUsed.isEmpty ? "Assistant" : node.modelUsed)
            Menu {
                Text("Regenerate with model:")
                ForEach(store.availableModels) { model in
                    Button(model.shortDisplayName) {
                        store.regenerateAssistantBranch(forAssistantNodeID: node.id, withOverrideModel: model.name)
                    }
                }
            } label: {
                HStack(spacing: 4) {
                    Text(shortLabel).font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText).lineLimit(1)
                    Image(systemName: "chevron.up.chevron.down").font(.system(size: 8.5, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                }
                .frame(maxWidth: maxLabelWidth, alignment: .leading)
            }
            .menuStyle(.borderlessButton)
            .frame(maxWidth: maxLabelWidth, alignment: .leading)
        }
    }

    private func branchPaginationControl(nodeID: UUID, siblingMeta: (currentIndex: Int, totalCount: Int)) -> some View {
        HStack(spacing: 2) {
            Button { store.switchBranchVersion(forNodeID: nodeID, delta: -1) } label: {
                Image(systemName: "chevron.left").font(.system(size: 10, weight: .bold)).frame(width: 20, height: 20)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.plain)
            .disabled(siblingMeta.currentIndex == 0)
            .opacity(siblingMeta.currentIndex == 0 ? 0.35 : 1.0)

            Text("\(siblingMeta.currentIndex + 1)/\(siblingMeta.totalCount)")
                .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                .padding(.horizontal, 2)

            Button { store.switchBranchVersion(forNodeID: nodeID, delta: 1) } label: {
                Image(systemName: "chevron.right").font(.system(size: 10, weight: .bold)).frame(width: 20, height: 20)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.plain)
            .disabled(siblingMeta.currentIndex >= siblingMeta.totalCount - 1)
            .opacity(siblingMeta.currentIndex >= siblingMeta.totalCount - 1 ? 0.35 : 1.0)
        }
        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
        .padding(.horizontal, 3)
        .frame(height: 22)
        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.elevatedSurface.opacity(0.75)))
        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func turnMainBody(
        node: ConversationMessageNode,
        isNodeStreaming: Bool,
        opticalFontSize: CGFloat,
        isFullscreenOrWide: Bool
    ) -> some View {
        if !node.attachments.isEmpty {
            ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                    ForEach(node.attachments) { att in
                        HStack(spacing: 5) {
                            Image(systemName: att.kind == .downscaledVisionImage ? "photo" : "doc.text").font(.system(size: 10))
                            Text("\(att.title) (\(att.formattedByteSize) · ~\(att.tokenEstimate) tok)")
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 8).padding(.vertical, 4)
                        .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)
                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous))
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if !node.thinkingTrace.isEmpty || (!node.isThinkingComplete && isNodeStreaming) {
            thinkingDisclosureView(for: node, isStreaming: isNodeStreaming, opticalFontSize: opticalFontSize)
        }

        if editingMessageNodeID == node.id {
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                MotifStyledTextEditor(text: $editMessageDraft, font: typo.nsFont(size: opticalFontSize), tokens: tokens)
                    .frame(height: isEditBoxExpandedFull ? 560 : 220)
                    .padding(8)
                    .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)
                    .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous))
                    .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.strongBorder, lineWidth: 1))

                HStack(spacing: 12) {
                    if node.role == .assistant && !node.thinkingTrace.isEmpty {
                        Toggle("Clear stored thinking", isOn: $clearThinkingOnAssistantEdit)
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    }
                    Spacer()
                    Button("Cancel") { editingMessageNodeID = nil }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 12))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)

                    if node.role == .user {
                        Button("Save & Resubmit") {
                            let updated = editMessageDraft
                            editingMessageNodeID = nil
                            store.createEditedMessageBranch(originalNodeID: node.id, updatedText: updated)
                        }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 4)
                        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    } else {
                        Button("Save Trimmed Reply") {
                            let updated = editMessageDraft
                            let clearThink = clearThinkingOnAssistantEdit
                            editingMessageNodeID = nil
                            store.updateAssistantMessageInPlace(nodeID: node.id, updatedText: updated, clearThinking: clearThink)
                        }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 4)
                        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    }
                }
            }
        } else if node.content.isEmpty && isNodeStreaming {
            if store.isLoadingModelWeights {
                let frac = min(0.99, max(0.02, store.memorySnapshot.loadProgressAgainstTarget))
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
                    Text("Loading \(store.loadingModelName) into Unified Memory (\(Int(frac * 100))%)…")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                    ProgressView(value: frac).tint(tokens.accentAction).frame(maxWidth: 260)
                }
            } else if store.isEvaluatingConversationContext && node.thinkingTrace.isEmpty {
                Text("Reading conversation context…").font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium)).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
            } else if node.thinkingTrace.isEmpty {
                Text("Generating…").font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .medium)).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
            }
        } else if !node.content.isEmpty {
            StreamSafeMarkdownView(
                rawMarkdown: isNodeStreaming ? (node.content + " ▍") : node.content,
                isStreaming: isNodeStreaming,
                effectiveFontSize: opticalFontSize,
                tokens: tokens,
                typo: typo,
                onOpenCodeInInspector: { _, _ in
                    store.userManuallyClosedRightPaneInWideMode = false
                    store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen = true
                }
            )
        }

        if let err = node.errorNote {
            Text(err).font(typo.sans(size: 12.5)).foregroundColor(.red)
        }
    }

    // Pure helper (no imperative mutation inside @ViewBuilder!)
    private func buildTelemetryFooter(node: ConversationMessageNode, session: ConversationSession, isLatestTurn: Bool) -> (text: String, isWarning: Bool) {
        let answerTok = node.role == .assistant
            ? (node.content.isEmpty ? 0 : max(node.estimatedTokenFootprint, node.metrics?.totalTokens ?? 0))
            : node.estimatedTokenFootprint
        let m = node.metrics
        let tps = m?.tokensPerSecond ?? 0.0
        let cached = m?.promptEvalCachedTokens ?? 0
        let promptTok = m?.promptEvalTokens ?? 0
        let doneReason = m?.doneReason ?? ""

        var parts: [String] = []
        if node.role == .assistant && tps > 0 {
            parts.append(String(format: "%.1f tok/s", tps))
            parts.append("\(answerTok) tok")
        } else {
            parts.append("~\(answerTok) tok")
        }

        if isLatestTurn {
            parts.append("Chat: \(session.estimatedActiveTokens) tok")
        }
        if node.role == .assistant && promptTok > 0 && cached > 0 {
            let pct = Int((Double(cached) / Double(promptTok)) * 100.0)
            parts.append("KV Hit \(pct)%")
        }
        if doneReason == "length" {
            parts.append("Truncated (limit)")
        }
        return (parts.joined(separator: " · "), doneReason == "length")
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func turnBottomActionBar(
        node: ConversationMessageNode,
        session: ConversationSession,
        isLatestTurn: Bool,
        isNodeStreaming: Bool
    ) -> some View {
        HStack(spacing: 14) {
            if isNodeStreaming {
                let isThinkingPhase = !node.isThinkingComplete && node.content.isEmpty
                HStack(spacing: 6) {
                    Circle().fill(tokens.accentAction).frame(width: 5, height: 5)
                    if isThinkingPhase {
                        Text(String(format: "%.1f tok/s · %d thinking tok", store.liveTokensPerSec, max(1, store.liveThinkingTokens)))
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    } else {
                        Text(String(format: "%.1f tok/s · %d tok", store.liveTokensPerSec, max(1, store.liveTotalTokens)))
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    }
                }
                Spacer()
                Text("Chat: \(session.estimatedActiveTokens) tok")
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
            } else {
                if confirmingDeleteNodeID == node.id {
                    HStack(spacing: 8) {
                        Text("Delete turn (~ \(node.estimatedTokenFootprint) tok)?")
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .semibold).monospacedDigit())
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                        Button("Confirm") {
                            confirmingDeleteNodeID = nil
                            store.deleteSingleMessageNode(node.id)
                        }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 8).padding(.vertical, 3)
                        .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 4, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        Button("Cancel") { confirmingDeleteNodeID = nil }
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                            .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    }
                } else {
                    Button {
                        NSPasteboard.general.clearContents()
                        NSPasteboard.general.setString(node.content.isEmpty ? node.thinkingTrace : node.content, forType: .string)
                        copiedMessageID = node.id
                        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 1.5) {
                            if copiedMessageID == node.id { copiedMessageID = nil }
                        }
                    } label: {
                        HStack(spacing: 4) {
                            Image(systemName: copiedMessageID == node.id ? "checkmark" : "doc.on.doc").font(.system(size: 10.5))
                            Text(copiedMessageID == node.id ? "Copied" : "Copy").font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)

                    Button {
                        editMessageDraft = node.content
                        clearThinkingOnAssistantEdit = false
                        editingMessageNodeID = node.id
                    } label: {
                        HStack(spacing: 4) {
                            Image(systemName: "pencil").font(.system(size: 10.5))
                            Text("Edit").font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)

                    if node.role == .user && node.childrenIDs.isEmpty && !store.isStreamingGeneration {
                        Button("Generate Reply") { store.regenerateOrAnswerUserNode(nodeID: node.id) }
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.accentAction)
                            .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    }

                    if node.role == .assistant {
                        Button { store.regenerateAssistantBranch(forAssistantNodeID: node.id) } label: {
                            HStack(spacing: 4) {
                                Image(systemName: "arrow.clockwise").font(.system(size: 10.5))
                                Text("Regenerate").font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                            }
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        }
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)

                        if node.wasStoppedMidStream && isLatestTurn {
                            Button("Continue") { store.continueStoppedGeneration(forAssistantNodeID: node.id) }
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold))
                                .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                                .padding(.horizontal, 8).padding(.vertical, 3)
                                .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 4, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        }
                    }

                    Button { store.branchMessageToNewChat(upToNodeID: node.id) } label: {
                        HStack(spacing: 4) {
                            Image(systemName: "arrow.triangle.branch").font(.system(size: 10.5))
                            Text("Branch").font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)

                    Button { confirmingDeleteNodeID = node.id } label: {
                        HStack(spacing: 3) {
                            Image(systemName: "trash").font(.system(size: 10))
                            Text("Delete").font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }

                Spacer()

                let footer = buildTelemetryFooter(node: node, session: session, isLatestTurn: isLatestTurn)
                Text(footer.text)
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                    .foregroundColor(footer.isWarning ? tokens.statusWarning : tokens.mutedText)
            }
        }
        .frame(height: 24)
        .padding(.top, 2)
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func thinkingDisclosureView(for node: ConversationMessageNode, isStreaming: Bool, opticalFontSize: CGFloat) -> some View {
        let relFont = max(10.5, opticalFontSize - 2.4)
        let headerFont = max(10.5, opticalFontSize - 3.0)
        let isActivelyThinking = !node.isThinkingComplete && isStreaming
        let thinkTok = isActivelyThinking ? max(store.liveThinkingTokens, node.thinkingTrace.count / 4) : max(node.metrics?.thinkingTokens ?? 0, node.thinkingTrace.count / 4)

        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 5) {
            Button {
                guard let sIdx = store.sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == store.selectedSessionID }),
                      var target = store.sessions[sIdx].nodes[node.id] else { return }
                withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.14)) {
                    target.isThinkingExpanded.toggle()
                    store.sessions[sIdx].nodes[node.id] = target
                }
            } label: {
                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                    RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 1.5, style: .continuous)
                        .fill(isActivelyThinking ? tokens.accentAction : tokens.thinkingText.opacity(0.6))
                        .frame(width: 6, height: 6)
                    Text(node.wasStoppedMidThinking ? "Thought Process · Stopped" : (isActivelyThinking ? "Thinking Live" : "Thought Process"))
                        .font(typo.sans(size: headerFont, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.thinkingText)
                    Text(node.isThinkingComplete ? String(format: "%.1fs", node.metrics?.thinkingDurationSec ?? 0.0) : String(format: "%.1fs…", store.liveElapsedSeconds))
                        .font(typo.sans(size: headerFont, weight: .medium).monospacedDigit())
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.thinkingText.opacity(0.75))
                    if !node.thinkingTrace.isEmpty {
                        Text("· ~\(max(1, thinkTok)) tok")
                            .font(typo.sans(size: headerFont).monospacedDigit())
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.thinkingText.opacity(0.70))
                    }
                    Image(systemName: "chevron.right")
                        .font(.system(size: 8.5, weight: .bold))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.thinkingText.opacity(0.75))
                        .rotationEffect(.degrees(node.isThinkingExpanded ? 90 : 0))
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 5.5)
                .background(tokens.thinkingSurface)
                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous))
                .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
            }
            .buttonStyle(.plain)

            if node.isThinkingExpanded {
                StreamSafeMarkdownView(
                    rawMarkdown: node.thinkingTrace.isEmpty ? "Reasoning…" : node.thinkingTrace,
                    isStreaming: isActivelyThinking,
                    effectiveFontSize: relFont,
                    tokens: tokens,
                    typo: typo,
                    useMonospaceBody: true,
                    overrideTextColor: tokens.thinkingText,
                    onOpenCodeInInspector: { _, _ in store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen = true }
                )
                .padding(12)
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                .background(tokens.thinkingSurface)
                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous))
                .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
            }
        }
        .padding(.bottom, 2)
    }
}
EOF

import SwiftUI
import AppKit

public struct ProgressiveComposerBar: View {
    @ObservedObject public var store: WorkstationStore
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    public var maxBarWidth: CGFloat = 740.0
    public var activeFontSize: CGFloat = 14.0
    public var isWideOrFullscreen: Bool = false

    @State private var isComposerFocused: Bool = false
    @State private var compactEditorHeight: CGFloat = 28.0
    @State private var wrappedLineCount: Int = 1

    private var typo: TypographyConfiguration { store.typography }
    private var isSendEnabled: Bool { store.isStreamingGeneration || store.sendDisabledReason == nil }
    private var matchingPersonas: [SlashPersona] { store.matchedSlashPersonas }
    private var effectiveEditorHeight: CGFloat { store.isComposerCanvasExpanded ? 240.0 : compactEditorHeight }
    private var adaptivePlaceholder: String {
        isWideOrFullscreen ? "Message Motif…  (Type / for personas · Shift+Return for new line)" : "Message Motif…  ( / for personas )"
    }

    public var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
            if !matchingPersonas.isEmpty {
                SlashPersonaCommandPaletteCard(
                    personas: matchingPersonas,

                    tokens: tokens,

                        store.settingsRequestedTab = "Slash Personas"
                        store.isSettingsModalPresented = true
                    },
                    onDismiss: { store.isSlashMenuDismissedForCurrentToken = true }
                )
                .frame(maxWidth: min(maxBarWidth, 500))
                .transition(.opacity.combined(with: .move(edge: .bottom)))
            }

            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 0) {

                                        .font(.system(size: 10))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)

                    IMESafeComposerTextView(
                        text: Binding(
                            get: { store.composerDraft },
                            set: { store.handleComposerTextChange($0) }
                        ),
                        isFocused: $isComposerFocused,
                        compactHeight: $compactEditorHeight,
                        lineCount: $wrappedLineCount,
                        placeholder: adaptivePlaceholder,
                        tokens: tokens,
                        typo: typo,
                        fontSize: activeFontSize,
                        isSlashMenuVisible: !matchingPersonas.isEmpty,
                        onSend: {

                                store.sendActivePrompt()
                            }
                        },
                        onSlashMoveUp: { store.moveSlashSelection(delta: -1) },
                        onSlashMoveDown: { store.moveSlashSelection(delta: 1) },
                        onSlashConfirm: { store.applyHighlightedSlashPersona() },
                        onSlashDismiss: { store.isSlashMenuDismissedForCurrentToken = true },
                        onLargePaste: { pasted in store.handleLargePasteAsChip(pasted) }
                    )
                    .frame(height: effectiveEditorHeight)

                    Button {
                        withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.16)) { store.isComposerCanvasExpanded.toggle() }
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: store.isComposerCanvasExpanded ? "arrow.down.right.and.arrow.up.left" : "arrow.up.left.and.arrow.down.right")
                            .font(.system(size: 10.5, weight: .semibold))
                            .foregroundColor(store.isComposerCanvasExpanded ? tokens.accentAction : tokens.secondaryText)
                            .frame(width: 24, height: 24)

                            .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    .padding(.top, 2)
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, 12).padding(.top, 8).padding(.bottom, 4)

                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 8)
                        .frame(height: 26)
                        .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)

                    if let activeTrigger = store.activeSession?.activePersonaTrigger {
                        HStack(spacing: 4) {
                            Text(activeTrigger).font(typo.mono(size: 10.5, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.accentAction)
                            Button {
                                if let idx = store.sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == store.selectedSessionID }) {
                                    store.sessions[idx].activePersonaTrigger = nil

                                Image(systemName: "xmark").font(.system(size: 8, weight: .bold)).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                            }
