                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                    }

                    if !store.composerDraft.isEmpty {
                        Text("~\(max(1, store.composerDraft.count / 4)) tok")
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5).monospacedDigit())
                            .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                    }

                    Spacer()

                    Button { store.isParameterPopoverPresented.toggle() } label: {
                        HStack(spacing: 5) {
                            Image(systemName: "slider.horizontal.3").font(.system(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                            Text("Parameters").font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 10).frame(height: 28)
                        .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
                        .clipShape(Capsule())
                        .overlay(Capsule().strokeBorder(tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    .popover(isPresented: $store.isParameterPopoverPresented, arrowEdge: .top) {
                        ParameterInspectorPopover(store: store, tokens: tokens)
                    }

                    Button {
                        if store.isStreamingGeneration { store.stopActiveGeneration() }
                        else { store.sendActivePrompt() }
                    } label: {
                        ZStack {
                            Circle().fill(isSendEnabled ? tokens.accentAction : tokens.hairline).frame(width: 30, height: 30)
                            if store.isStreamingGeneration {
                                RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 2, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentForeground).frame(width: 9, height: 9)
                            } else {
                                Image(systemName: "arrow.up")
                                    .font(.system(size: 12.5, weight: .bold))
                                    .foregroundColor(isSendEnabled ? tokens.accentForeground : tokens.mutedText)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    .disabled(!isSendEnabled)
                    .help(store.isStreamingGeneration ? "Stop generation" : (store.sendDisabledReason ?? "Send message"))
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 6)
            }
            .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)
            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 14, style: .continuous))
            .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 14, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(isComposerFocused ? tokens.strongBorder : tokens.hairline, lineWidth: 1))
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: maxBarWidth)
    }

    private func attachLocalFileOrVisionAsset() {
        let panel = NSOpenPanel()
        panel.title = "Attach File or Image"
        panel.allowsMultipleSelection = true
        panel.canChooseDirectories = false
        if panel.runModal() == .OK { store.attachFilesFromURLs(panel.urls) }
    }
}

public struct SlashPersonaCommandPaletteCard: View {
    public let personas: [SlashPersona]
    public let highlightedIndex: Int
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    public let typo: TypographyConfiguration
    public let onSelect: (SlashPersona) -> Void
    public let onManagePersonas: () -> Void
    public let onDismiss: () -> Void

    public var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 0) {
            HStack(spacing: 8) {
                Text("SLASH PERSONAS").font(typo.sans(size: 10, weight: .semibold)).tracking(0.6).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                Spacer()
                Text("↑↓ · ↵ · esc").font(typo.sans(size: 10)).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                Button(action: onDismiss) {
                    Image(systemName: "xmark").font(.system(size: 9.5, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 14).frame(height: 30)
            .background(tokens.elevatedSurface.opacity(0.55))

            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

            ScrollView(.vertical, showsIndicators: true) {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                    ForEach(Array(personas.enumerated()), id: \.element.id) { idx, persona in
                        let isHighlighted = idx == highlightedIndex
                        Button { onSelect(persona) } label: {
                            HStack(spacing: 10) {
                                Text(persona.trigger)
                                    .font(typo.mono(size: 11, weight: .semibold))
                                    .foregroundColor(isHighlighted ? tokens.accentForeground : tokens.accentAction)
                                    .padding(.horizontal, 7).padding(.vertical, 3)
                                    .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous).fill(isHighlighted ? tokens.accentAction : tokens.elevatedSurface))
                                    .frame(width: 92, alignment: .leading)
                                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                                    Text(persona.title).font(typo.sans(size: 12.5, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText).lineLimit(1)
                                    Text(persona.systemPrompt).font(typo.sans(size: 11)).foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText).lineLimit(1)
                                }
                                Spacer(minLength: 4)
                            }
                            .padding(.horizontal, 10).padding(.vertical, 6)
                            .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 7, style: .continuous).fill(isHighlighted ? tokens.selectionPill : Color.clear))
                            .contentShape(Rectangle())
                        }
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    }
                }
                .padding(6)
            }
            .frame(maxHeight: 210)

            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

            HStack {
                Button("Create or edit personas in Settings…", action: onManagePersonas)
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                Spacer()
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 14).frame(height: 28)
        }
        .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 11, style: .continuous))
        .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 11, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(tokens.strongBorder, lineWidth: 1))
        .shadow(color: Color.black.opacity(0.14), radius: 12, y: 4)
    }
}

public struct ModelPickerPopoverView: View {
    @ObservedObject public var store: WorkstationStore
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    private var typo: TypographyConfiguration { store.typography }

    public var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            HStack {
                Text("Local Models").font(typo.sans(size: 12.5, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                Spacer()
                Button("Refresh") {
                    Task {
                        await store.refreshLocalEngineModels()
                        await store.pollOllamaRunningProcesses()
                    }
                }
                .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5)).foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText).buttonStyle(.plain)
            }

            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

            if store.availableModels.isEmpty {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                    Text("No models found in Ollama.").font(typo.sans(size: 12.5)).foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    Button("Open Settings to Pull Models…") {
                        store.isModelPickerPopoverPresented = false
                        store.settingsRequestedTab = "Models & Storage"
                        store.isSettingsModalPresented = true
                    }
                    .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold)).buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
            } else {
                ScrollView(.vertical, showsIndicators: false) {
                    VStack(spacing: 4) {
                        ForEach(store.availableModels) { model in
                            let isSelected = model.name == store.activeModelDescriptor?.name
                            let isLoaded = model.residency == .activeInUnifiedRAM

                            HStack(spacing: 8) {
                                Circle().fill(isLoaded ? tokens.statusOnline : tokens.mutedText).frame(width: 6, height: 6)

                                Button {
                                    store.selectModelOnly(model)
                                    store.isModelPickerPopoverPresented = false
                                } label: {
                                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                                        HStack(spacing: 5) {
                                            Text(model.shortDisplayName)
                                                .font(typo.sans(size: 12.5, weight: isSelected ? .semibold : .regular))
                                                .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                                .lineLimit(1)
                                            if model.supportsVision {
                                                Text("VISION").font(typo.mono(size: 8.5, weight: .bold))
                                                    .padding(.horizontal, 4).padding(.vertical, 1)
                                                    .background(tokens.elevatedSurface).clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 3))
                                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                            }
                                            if model.supportsTools {
                                                Text("TOOLS").font(typo.mono(size: 8.5, weight: .bold))
                                                    .padding(.horizontal, 4).padding(.vertical, 1)
                                                    .background(tokens.elevatedSurface).clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 3))
                                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                            }
                                        }
                                        let details = [
                                            model.parameterSize,
                                            model.quantizationLevel,
                                            model.formattedSize,
                                            model.loadedContextLength.map { "\($0 / 1024)k loaded" } ?? ""
                                        ].filter { !$0.isEmpty }
                                        Text(details.joined(separator: " · "))
                                            .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5).monospacedDigit())
                                            .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                    }
                                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                                    .contentShape(Rectangle())
                                }
                                .buttonStyle(.plain)

                                if isLoaded {
                                    Button("Unload") { Task { await store.unloadModelFromMemory(model) } }
                                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                                        .foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                                        .padding(.horizontal, 8).padding(.vertical, 3)
                                        .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
                                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous))
                                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                } else {
                                    Button("Load") {
                                        store.selectAndLoadModel(model)
                                        store.isModelPickerPopoverPresented = false
                                    }
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .semibold))
                                    .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                                    .padding(.horizontal, 8).padding(.vertical, 3)
                                    .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                }
                            }
                            .padding(.horizontal, 8).padding(.vertical, 6)
                            .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(isSelected ? tokens.selectionPill : Color.clear))
                        }
                    }
                }
                .frame(maxHeight: 320)
            }
        }
        .padding(14)
        .frame(width: 390)
        .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
    }
}

public struct ParameterInspectorPopover: View {
    @ObservedObject public var store: WorkstationStore
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    private var typo: TypographyConfiguration { store.typography }

    public var body: some View {
        if let sIdx = store.sessions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == store.selectedSessionID }) {
            let binding = $store.sessions[sIdx]
            let model = store.activeModelDescriptor

            ScrollView(.vertical, showsIndicators: true) {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
                    HStack {
                        Text("Session Parameters").font(typo.sans(size: 13, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                        Spacer()
                        Button("Reset Sampling") {
                            binding.wrappedValue.parameters.temperature = nil
                            binding.wrappedValue.parameters.topP = nil
                            binding.wrappedValue.parameters.topK = nil
                            binding.wrappedValue.parameters.minP = nil
                            binding.wrappedValue.parameters.repeatPenalty = nil
                            store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference()
                        }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.accentAction)
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    }

                    HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                    if let m = model {
                        let profile = m.thinkingProfile
                        if profile.kind != .unsupported || binding.wrappedValue.parameters.forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported {
                            let opts = binding.wrappedValue.parameters.forceThinkingOverrideWhenUnsupported
                                ? [ThinkingModeOption.off, .on, .level("low"), .level("medium"), .level("high")]
                                : profile.validOptions
                            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                                Text("Thinking Mode").font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                Picker("", selection: binding.parameters.thinkingMode) {
                                    ForEach(opts) { mode in Text(mode.displayLabel).tag(mode) }
                                }
                                .pickerStyle(.segmented).labelsHidden()
                                .onChange(of: binding.wrappedValue.parameters.thinkingMode) { _, _ in store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
                            }
                        }

                        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                            HStack {
                                Text("Context Window (num_ctx)").font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                                Spacer()
                                Text("~\(String(format: "%.2f GB", store.memorySnapshot.kvCacheGB)) KV")
                                    .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5).monospacedDigit()).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                            }
                            let tiers = [2048, 4096, 8192, 16384, 32768, 65536].filter { $0 <= max(4096, m.maxContextLength) }
                            Picker("", selection: binding.parameters.numCtx) {
                                ForEach(tiers, id: \.self) { t in Text("\(t / 1024)k").tag(t) }
                            }
                            .pickerStyle(.segmented).labelsHidden()
                            .onChange(of: binding.wrappedValue.parameters.numCtx) { _, _ in
                                store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference()
                                store.recalculateHardwareTelemetry(sampleKernelRSS: false)
                            }
                            Text("Changing num_ctx mid-chat triggers a one-time model reload on the next turn.")
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 10)).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                        }
                    }

                    HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                    OptionalParameterSliderRow(title: "Temperature", explanation: "Nil preserves model's native Modelfile temperature.", value: binding.parameters.temperature, fallbackValue: 0.70, range: 0.0...2.0, tokens: tokens, typo: typo) { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
                    OptionalParameterSliderRow(title: "Top-P", explanation: "Nucleus sampling.", value: binding.parameters.topP, fallbackValue: 0.90, range: 0.05...1.0, tokens: tokens, typo: typo) { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
                    OptionalParameterSliderRow(title: "Min-P", explanation: "Relative probability floor.", value: binding.parameters.minP, fallbackValue: 0.05, range: 0.0...0.5, tokens: tokens, typo: typo) { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }
                    OptionalParameterSliderRow(title: "Repeat Penalty", explanation: "Repetition penalty.", value: binding.parameters.repeatPenalty, fallbackValue: 1.10, range: 0.8...1.6, tokens: tokens, typo: typo) { store.recordActiveModelParameterPreference() }

                    HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                    Toggle("Enforce JSON / Structured Schema", isOn: binding.parameters.enforceJSONMode)
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)

                    if binding.wrappedValue.parameters.enforceJSONMode {
                        Text("Optional JSON Schema (leave blank for generic JSON mode):")
                            .font(typo.sans(size: 10.5)).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                        MotifStyledTextEditor(text: binding.parameters.jsonSchemaText, font: NSFont.monospacedSystemFont(ofSize: 11, weight: .regular), tokens: tokens)
                            .frame(height: 84)
                            .padding(4)
                            .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)
                            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
                    }
                }
                .padding(14)
            }
            .frame(width: 350, height: 470)
            .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
        } else {
            Text("No active session").padding(20)
        }
    }
}

public struct ArtifactInspectorPane: View {
    @ObservedObject public var store: WorkstationStore
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    private var typo: TypographyConfiguration { store.typography }

    private var artifacts: [WorkspaceArtifact] { store.activeSession?.artifacts ?? [] }
    private var selectedArtifact: WorkspaceArtifact? {
        if let id = store.selectedArtifactID, let found = artifacts.first(where: { $0.id == id }) { return found }
        return artifacts.last
    }

    public var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 0) {
            HStack {
                Text("Code Inspector").font(typo.sans(size: 13, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                Spacer()
                Button {
                    store.userManuallyClosedRightPaneInWideMode = true
                    store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen = false
                } label: {
                    Image(systemName: "xmark").font(.system(size: 10.5, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 14)
            .frame(height: 44)

            HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

            if artifacts.isEmpty {
                VStack(spacing: 8) {
                    Spacer()
                    Text("No code artifacts yet").font(typo.sans(size: 12.5, weight: .medium)).foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                    Text("Fenced code blocks generated in this chat appear here for quick copy and export.")
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                        .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 20)
                    Spacer()
                }
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
            } else {
                ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                    HStack(spacing: 6) {
                        ForEach(artifacts) { art in
                            let isSel = art.id == selectedArtifact?.id
                            Button(art.title) { store.selectedArtifactID = art.id }
                                .font(typo.sans(size: 11, weight: isSel ? .semibold : .regular))
                                .foregroundColor(isSel ? tokens.accentForeground : tokens.secondaryText)
                                .padding(.horizontal, 9).padding(.vertical, 4)
                                .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 5, style: .continuous).fill(isSel ? tokens.accentAction : tokens.elevatedSurface))
                                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(10)
                }

                HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                if let art = selectedArtifact {
                    ScrollView([.vertical, .horizontal], showsIndicators: true) {
                        Text(CodeSyntaxHighlighter.highlight(code: art.content, isDark: tokens.isEffectivelyDark, baseColor: tokens.primaryText))
                            .lineSpacing(3.5)
                            .textSelection(.enabled)
                            .padding(14)
                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                    }
                    .background(tokens.canvasBackground)

                    HairlineDivider(tokens: tokens)

                    HStack {
                        Text(art.language.uppercased()).font(typo.mono(size: 10, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.mutedText)
                        Spacer()
                        Button("Copy Code") {
                            NSPasteboard.general.clearContents()
                            NSPasteboard.general.setString(art.content, forType: .string)
                        }
                        .font(typo.sans(size: 11.5, weight: .medium))
                        .foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal, 14).frame(height: 36)
                }
            }
        }
        .frame(width: 360)
        .background(tokens.sidebarBackground)
        .overlay(alignment: .leading) { HairlineDivider(axis: .vertical, tokens: tokens) }
    }
}

    private var slashPersonasTab: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            Text("Slash Personas").font(typo.sans(size: 13, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)

                    Spacer()
                    Button("Remove") {
                        store.slashPersonas.removeAll { $0.id == persona.id }
                        store.saveAllState()

                    .font(typo.sans(size: 11)).foregroundColor(.red).buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
                .padding(10)
                .background(tokens.elevatedSurface)

                TextField("/trigger", text: $newPersonaTrigger).textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder).frame(width: 120)
                TextField("Title", text: $newPersonaTitle).textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)
            }
            TextField("System prompt instructions…", text: $newPersonaPrompt).textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)
            Button("Add Persona") {
                let trig = newPersonaTrigger.hasPrefix("/") ? newPersonaTrigger : "/\(newPersonaTrigger)"
                guard trig.count > 1, !newPersonaTitle.isEmpty, !newPersonaPrompt.isEmpty else { return }
                store.slashPersonas.append(SlashPersona(trigger: trig, title: newPersonaTitle, systemPrompt: newPersonaPrompt))
                store.saveAllState()
                newPersonaTrigger = ""
                newPersonaTitle = ""
                newPersonaPrompt = ""
            }
            .font(typo.sans(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
            .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
            .padding(.horizontal, 12).padding(.vertical, 5)
            .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
            .buttonStyle(.plain)
        }
    }
}

public struct SimpleNamePromptSheet: View {
    public let title: String
    public let placeholder: String
    public let confirmLabel: String
    public let tokens: MotifTokens
    public let onConfirm: (String) -> Void
    public let onCancel: () -> Void
    @State private var input: String = ""

    public var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
            Text(title).font(.system(size: 14, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(tokens.primaryText)
            TextField(placeholder, text: $input)
                .textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)
                .onSubmit {
                    if !input.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty { onConfirm(input) }
                }
            HStack {
                Spacer()
                Button("Cancel", action: onCancel).buttonStyle(.plain).foregroundColor(tokens.secondaryText)
                Button(confirmLabel) { onConfirm(input) }
                    .foregroundColor(tokens.accentForeground)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 12).padding(.vertical, 5)
                    .background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous).fill(tokens.accentAction))
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    .disabled(input.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty)
            }
        }
        .padding(18)
        .frame(width: 340)
        .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
    }
}

cat << 'EOF' > Motif/Sources/Motif/Views/MainWorkstationView.swift
import SwiftUI
import AppKit

public struct MainWorkstationView: View {
    @ObservedObject public var store: WorkstationStore

    public var body: some View {
        let tokens = store.currentTokens

        HStack(spacing: 0) {
            if store.isSidebarVisible {
                SidebarNavigationView(store: store, tokens: tokens)
                    .frame(width: store.sidebarWidth)
            }

            EditorialConversationView(store: store, tokens: tokens)
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, maxHeight: .infinity)

            if store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen {
                ArtifactInspectorPane(store: store, tokens: tokens)
            }
        }
        .background(tokens.canvasBackground)
        .preferredColorScheme(tokens.isEffectivelyDark ? .dark : .light)
        .sheet(isPresented: $store.isSettingsModalPresented) {
            SettingsModalView(store: store, tokens: tokens)
        }
        .sheet(isPresented: $store.isNewWorkspaceSheetPresented) {
            SimpleNamePromptSheet(
                title: "New Workspace",
                placeholder: "Workspace name",
                confirmLabel: "Create",
                tokens: tokens,
                onConfirm: { name in
                    store.createWorkspace(name: name)
                    store.isNewWorkspaceSheetPresented = false
                },
                onCancel: { store.isNewWorkspaceSheetPresented = false }
            )
        }
        .sheet(isPresented: $store.isNewPresetSheetPresented) {
            SimpleNamePromptSheet(
                title: "Save Parameter Preset",
                placeholder: "Preset name",
                confirmLabel: "Save",
                tokens: tokens,
                onConfirm: { name in
                    store.createPreset(name: name)
                    store.isNewPresetSheetPresented = false
                },
                onCancel: { store.isNewPresetSheetPresented = false }
            )
        }
        .alert(
            "Model Exceeds Available Unified Memory",
            isPresented: Binding(
                get: { store.pendingSSDSwapWarningModel != nil },
                set: { if !$0 { store.pendingSSDSwapWarningModel = nil } }
            ),
            presenting: store.pendingSSDSwapWarningModel
        ) { model in
            Button("Apply Tight-RAM & Load") {
                store.pendingSSDSwapWarningModel = nil
                store.applyTightRAMOptimization(andLoadModel: model)
            }
            Button("Load Anyway", role: .destructive) {
                store.selectAndLoadModel(model, bypassSwapGuard: true)
            }
            Button("Cancel", role: .cancel) {
                store.pendingSSDSwapWarningModel = nil
            }
        } message: { model in
            Text("“\(model.shortDisplayName)” (\(model.formattedSize)) plus its KV cache exceeds your current free Unified Memory (\(store.memorySnapshot.formattedHeadroom)). Loading it may cause heavy macOS SSD swap.")
        }
    }
}
EOF

cat << 'EOF' > Motif/Sources/Motif/MotifApp.swift
import SwiftUI
import AppKit

@main
public struct MotifApp: App {
    @StateObject private var store: WorkstationStore

    public init() {
        CustomFontRegistrar.registerBundledFonts()
        _store = StateObject(wrappedValue: WorkstationStore())
    }

    public var body: some Scene {
        WindowGroup {
            MainWorkstationView(store: store)
                .frame(minWidth: 820, minHeight: 560)
        }
        .windowStyle(.hiddenTitleBar)
        .commands {
            CommandGroup(replacing: .newItem) {
                Button("New Chat") { store.createNewSession(folderID: nil) }
                    .keyboardShortcut("n", modifiers: .command)
            }
            CommandMenu("Workstation") {
                Button("Toggle Sidebar") { store.isSidebarVisible.toggle() }
                    .keyboardShortcut("s", modifiers: .command)
                Button("Toggle Code Inspector") { store.isRightUtilityPaneOpen.toggle() }
                    .keyboardShortcut("\\", modifiers: .command)
                Divider()
                Button("Stop Generation") { store.stopActiveGeneration() }
                    .keyboardShortcut(".", modifiers: .command)
                    .disabled(!store.isStreamingGeneration)
                Divider()
                Button("Increase Text Size") { store.adjustActiveFontSize(delta: 0.5) }
                    .keyboardShortcut("+", modifiers: .command)
                Button("Decrease Text Size") { store.adjustActiveFontSize(delta: -0.5) }
                    .keyboardShortcut("-", modifiers: .command)
            }
        }
    }
}
EOF
)

printf "\n===== [15/17] Compile Release Universal/Apple Silicon Binary =====\n"
(
  cd Motif
  swift build -c release
)

printf "\n===== [16/17] Assemble Signed macOS Motif.app Bundle =====\n"
(
  APP_BUNDLE="build/dmg_staging/Motif.app"
  rm -rf "$APP_BUNDLE"
  mkdir -p "$APP_BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS"
  mkdir -p "$APP_BUNDLE/Contents/Resources"

  BIN_PATH="$(cd Motif && swift build -c release --show-bin-path)"
  cp "$BIN_PATH/Motif" "$APP_BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS/Motif"
  chmod +x "$APP_BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS/Motif"

  if [ -d "$BIN_PATH/Motif_Motif.bundle" ]; then
    cp -R "$BIN_PATH/Motif_Motif.bundle" "$APP_BUNDLE/Contents/Resources/"
  fi
  if [ -d "Motif/Sources/Motif/Resources/Fonts" ]; then
    cp -R "Motif/Sources/Motif/Resources/Fonts" "$APP_BUNDLE/Contents/Resources/"
  fi
  if [ -f "build/AppIcon.icns" ]; then
    cp "build/AppIcon.icns" "$APP_BUNDLE/Contents/Resources/AppIcon.icns"
  fi

  cat << 'PLIST' > "$APP_BUNDLE/Contents/Info.plist"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "[http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd](http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd)">
<plist version="1.0">
<dict>
  <key>CFBundleDevelopmentRegion</key>
  <string>en</string>
  <key>CFBundleExecutable</key>
  <string>Motif</string>
  <key>CFBundleIconFile</key>
  <string>AppIcon</string>
  <key>CFBundleIdentifier</key>
  <string>com.motif.studio.ollama</string>
  <key>CFBundleInfoDictionaryVersion</key>
  <string>6.0</string>
  <key>CFBundleName</key>
  <string>Motif</string>
  <key>CFBundlePackageType</key>
  <string>APPL</string>
  <key>CFBundleShortVersionString</key>
  <string>14.0.0</string>
  <key>CFBundleVersion</key>
  <string>1400</string>
  <key>LSMinimumSystemVersion</key>
  <string>14.0</string>
  <key>NSHighResolutionCapable</key>
  <true/>
  <key>NSAppTransportSecurity</key>
  <dict>
    <key>NSAllowsLocalNetworking</key>
    <true/>
  </dict>
</dict>
</plist>
PLIST

  codesign --force --deep --sign - "$APP_BUNDLE"
  ls -la "$APP_BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS/Motif"
)

printf "\n===== [17/17] Package Motif-14.0.0.dmg =====\n"
(
  rm -f build/Motif-14.0.0.dmg
  ln -sf /Applications build/dmg_staging/Applications
  hdiutil create \
    -volname "Motif" \
    -srcfolder build/dmg_staging \
    -ov \
    -format UDZO \
    build/Motif-14.0.0.dmg
  ls -lh build/Motif-14.0.0.dmg
  printf "\nBuild complete: build/Motif-14.0.0.dmg\n"
)
